FICHIER : lancer_contacts.py

# Commandes pip à exécuter : Aucune dépendance externe requise

import argparse
import os
import sys

ROOT_DIR: str = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
if ROOT_DIR not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, ROOT_DIR)

from common.core_utils import SharedCoreUtils
from google_contacts_parser.config_manager import ContactsConfig
from google_contacts_parser.pipeline_runner import ContactsPipelineRunner


class RootContactsLauncher:
    """
    Encapsule l'analyse des arguments et l'exécution du pipeline
    de contacts depuis la racine du dépôt.
    """

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        """
        Initialise le répertoire racine du projet.
        """
        self.root_dir: str = ROOT_DIR

    @staticmethod
    def parse_args() -> argparse.Namespace:
        """
        Analyse les arguments de ligne de commande pour le lanceur contacts.

        :return: Espace de noms contenant le chemin du fichier de configuration.
        """
        parser: argparse.ArgumentParser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
            description=(
                "Lance le pipeline d'extraction et d'anonymisation des contacts "
                "(google_contacts_parser) depuis la racine."
            )
        )
        parser.add_argument(
            "-c", "--config",
            default="config.ini",
            help="Chemin vers le fichier config.ini (défaut : config.ini).",
        )
        return parser.parse_args()

    def run(self) -> None:
        """
        Résout config.ini et exécute l'ensemble du pipeline de contacts.
        """
        args: argparse.Namespace = self.parse_args()
        config_path: str = SharedCoreUtils.resolve_config_path(args.config, self.root_dir)
        config: ContactsConfig = ContactsConfig(config_path=config_path)
        runner: ContactsPipelineRunner = ContactsPipelineRunner(config=config)
        runner.run_all()


def main() -> None:
    """
    Point d'entrée racine pour exécuter google_contacts_parser.
    """
    RootContactsLauncher().run()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    try:
        main()
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        print(
            "\n[ARRÊT] Traitement des contacts interrompu par l'utilisateur "
            "(Ctrl+C). Aucun fichier final modifié.",
            flush=True,
        )
        sys.exit(130)

================================================================================
FICHIER : lancer_documents.py
================================================================================
# Commandes pip à exécuter : Aucune dépendance externe requise

import argparse
import os
import sys

ROOT_DIR: str = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
if ROOT_DIR not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, ROOT_DIR)

from common.core_utils import SharedCoreUtils
from doc_anonymizer.anonymization_runner import DocumentAnonymizationRunner
from doc_anonymizer.config_manager import PipelineConfig
from doc_anonymizer.pipeline_runner import DocAnonymizerPipelineRunner
from tools.completer_contacts_manuels import ManualContactsAutoCompleter
from tools.sync_contacts import ContactsSynchronizer


class RootDocumentsLauncher:
    """
    Encapsule l'analyse des arguments et l'exécution du pipeline
    de documents depuis la racine du dépôt.
    """

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        """
        Initialise le répertoire racine du projet.
        """
        self.root_dir: str = ROOT_DIR

    @staticmethod
    def parse_args() -> argparse.Namespace:
        """
        Analyse les arguments de ligne de commande pour le lanceur documents.

        :return: Espace de noms contenant les options d'exécution.
        """
        parser: argparse.ArgumentParser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
            description=(
                "Lance le pipeline d'extraction et d'anonymisation de documents "
                "(doc_anonymizer) depuis la racine."
            )
        )
        parser.add_argument(
            "-c", "--config",
            default="config.ini",
            help="Chemin vers le fichier config.ini (défaut : config.ini).",
        )
        parser.add_argument(
            "--sync-contacts",
            default=None,
            help="Chemin d'un contacts.txt externe à fusionner avant traitement.",
        )
        parser.add_argument(
            "--overwrite-contacts",
            action="store_true",
            help="Écrase totalement contacts.txt lors de la synchronisation.",
        )
        parser.add_argument(
            "--allow-sleep",
            action="store_true",
            help="Autorise le système à se mettre en veille pendant l'exécution.",
        )
        parser.add_argument(
            "--complete-manual",
            action="store_true",
            help="Complète automatiquement les lignes vides 'Prénom Nom = ' dans contacts_manuels.txt.",
        )
        parser.add_argument(
            "--dry-run",
            action="store_true",
            help="Prévisualise les complétions de contacts_manuels.txt sans écrire sur le disque.",
        )
        parser.add_argument(
            "--manual-only",
            action="store_true",
            help="Applique uniquement contacts_manuels.txt sur resultats_anonymisation.",
        )
        return parser.parse_args()

    def run(self) -> None:
        """
        Exécute les synchronisations éventuelles puis l'étape demandée.
        """
        args: argparse.Namespace = self.parse_args()
        config_path: str = SharedCoreUtils.resolve_config_path(args.config, self.root_dir)
        config: PipelineConfig = PipelineConfig(config_path=config_path)

        if args.sync_contacts:
            print("=== SYNCHRONISATION DE CONTACTS.TXT ===", flush=True)
            ContactsSynchronizer.merge_and_sync(
                source_path=args.sync_contacts,
                dest_path=config.contacts_file,
                preserve_manual_rules=not args.overwrite_contacts,
            )
            print("", flush=True)

        if args.complete_manual:
            completer: ManualContactsAutoCompleter = ManualContactsAutoCompleter(
                config=config, dry_run=args.dry_run
            )
            completer.process()
            if not args.manual_only:
                return
            if args.dry_run:
                print(
                    "\n[INFO] Mode --dry-run actif : passe --manual-only "
                    "ignorée tant que les règles ne sont pas enregistrées.",
                    flush=True,
                )
                return
            print("", flush=True)

        if args.manual_only:
            manual_runner: DocumentAnonymizationRunner = DocumentAnonymizationRunner(
                config=config, manual_only=True
            )
            manual_runner.run_on_existing_results()
            return

        runner: DocAnonymizerPipelineRunner = DocAnonymizerPipelineRunner(
            config=config,
            prevent_sleep=not args.allow_sleep,
        )
        runner.run_all()


def main() -> None:
    """
    Point d'entrée racine pour exécuter doc_anonymizer.
    """
    RootDocumentsLauncher().run()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    try:
        main()
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        print(
            "\n[ARRÊT] Pipeline interrompu proprement par l'utilisateur "
            "(Ctrl+C). Fermeture immédiate.",
            flush=True,
        )
        sys.stdout.flush()
        sys.stderr.flush()
        os._exit(130)

# Commandes pip à exécuter : Aucune dépendance externe requise

"""
Package commun fournissant les utilitaires partagés de normalisation,
d'écriture atomique et de résolution de configuration.
"""

================================================================================
FICHIER : common/core_utils.py
================================================================================
# Commandes pip à exécuter : Aucune dépendance externe requise

import os
import re
import tempfile
import unicodedata
from typing import Set


class SharedCoreUtils:
    """
    Centralise les utilitaires partagés entre google_contacts_parser et doc_anonymizer :
    normalisation Unicode, identifiants (téléphones/e-mails), écriture atomique
    et résolution des chemins de configuration.
    """

    @staticmethod
    def strip_accents(text: str) -> str:
        """
        Supprime les diacritiques et accents d'une chaîne Unicode via NFD.

        :param text: Texte source brut.
        :return: Texte sans accents.
        """
        return "".join(
            ch
            for ch in unicodedata.normalize("NFD", text)
            if unicodedata.category(ch) != "Mn"
        )

    @classmethod
    def normalize_token(cls, text: str) -> str:
        """
        Normalise un jeton en minuscules sans accents ni espaces périphériques.

        :param text: Chaîne brute.
        :return: Chaîne normalisée.
        """
        return cls.strip_accents(text).lower().strip()

    @classmethod
    def normalize_compact_text(cls, text: str) -> str:
        """
        Normalise un nom en minuscules sans accents, espaces, tirets ni apostrophes.

        :param text: Texte brut à comparer.
        :return: Chaîne compacte normalisée.
        """
        base: str = cls.normalize_token(text)
        return re.sub(r"[\s\-'\u2019]", "", base)

    @staticmethod
    def normalize_email_alias(email: str, lowercase: bool = True) -> str:
        """
        Retire l'extension d'alias '+...' dans la partie locale d'un e-mail.

        :param email: Adresse e-mail brute.
        :param lowercase: Convertit l'adresse en minuscules si True.
        :return: Adresse e-mail canonique.
        """
        cleaned: str = re.sub(r"^mailto:", "", email.strip(), flags=re.IGNORECASE)
        if "@" not in cleaned:
            return cleaned
        local_part, domain_part = cleaned.rsplit("@", 1)
        if "+" in local_part:
            local_part, _ = local_part.split("+", 1)
        result: str = f"{local_part}@{domain_part}"
        return result.lower() if lowercase else result
    @classmethod
    def normalize_identifier(cls, value: str, lowercase_email: bool = True) -> str:
        """
        Uniformise un numéro de téléphone français (format 0XXXXXXXXX)
        ou international ainsi qu'une adresse e-mail.

        :param value: Coordonnée brute (téléphone ou e-mail).
        :param lowercase_email: Met l'e-mail en minuscules si True.
        :return: Identifiant normalisé.
        """
        val: str = value.strip()
        if "@" in val:
            return cls.normalize_email_alias(val, lowercase=lowercase_email)
        num: str = re.sub(r"[\s.\-\u00a0()]", "", val)
        if num.startswith("+330"):
            num = "0" + num[4:]
        elif num.startswith("+33"):
            num = "0" + num[3:]
        elif num.startswith("00330"):
            num = "0" + num[5:]
        elif num.startswith("0033"):
            num = "0" + num[4:]
        return num

    @staticmethod
    def atomic_write_text(target_path: str, content: str, encoding: str = "utf-8") -> None:
        """
        Écrit un fichier texte de façon atomique via un fichier temporaire
        dans le répertoire cible afin d'éviter toute corruption.

        :param target_path: Chemin final du fichier sur disque.
        :param content: Contenu complet à enregistrer.
        :param encoding: Encodage cible (défaut : utf-8).
        """
        parent_dir: str = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(target_path))
        if parent_dir:
            os.makedirs(parent_dir, exist_ok=True)
        fd, tmp_path = tempfile.mkstemp(
            dir=parent_dir, prefix=".tmp_atom_", suffix=".tmp"
        )
        try:
            with os.fdopen(fd, "w", encoding=encoding, newline="\n") as f_tmp:
                f_tmp.write(content)
            os.replace(tmp_path, target_path)
        except Exception:
            if os.path.exists(tmp_path):
                try:
                    os.remove(tmp_path)
                except OSError:
                    pass
            raise

    @staticmethod
    def resolve_config_path(raw_config: str, root_dir: str) -> str:
        """
        Résout le chemin vers config.ini en vérifiant le chemin fourni
        puis la racine du dépôt.

        :param raw_config: Chemin brut passé en argument CLI.
        :param root_dir: Répertoire racine du projet.
        :return: Chemin absolu existant vers config.ini.
        """
        if os.path.isabs(raw_config):
            if os.path.exists(raw_config):
                return raw_config
            raise FileNotFoundError(f"Fichier de configuration introuvable : '{raw_config}'")

        root_candidate: str = os.path.normpath(os.path.join(root_dir, raw_config))
        if os.path.exists(root_candidate):
            return root_candidate

        cwd_candidate: str = os.path.abspath(raw_config)
        if os.path.exists(cwd_candidate):
            return cwd_candidate

        raise FileNotFoundError(
            f"Fichier de configuration '{raw_config}' introuvable "
            f"(cherché dans '{root_candidate}' et '{cwd_candidate}')."
        )

    @classmethod
    def load_first_names_set(cls, file_path: str) -> Set[str]:
        """
        Charge et normalise les prénoms depuis le fichier prenoms.txt partagé.

        :param file_path: Chemin vers prenoms.txt.
        :return: Ensemble des prénoms normalisés sans accents.
        """
        names: Set[str] = set()
        if not os.path.exists(file_path):
            return names
        with open(file_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f_in:
            for raw_line in f_in:
                cleaned: str = raw_line.strip()
                if cleaned and not cleaned.startswith("#"):
                    norm: str = cls.normalize_token(cleaned)
                    if norm:
                        names.add(norm)
        return names

    @classmethod
    def load_locations_set(cls, file_path: str) -> Set[str]:
        """
        Charge les villes et communes supplémentaires depuis un fichier texte
        (une entrée par ligne ou séparée par des virgules, commentaires '#' ignorés).

        :param file_path: Chemin vers villes_masquees.txt.
        :return: Ensemble des noms de lieux normalisés sans accents.
        """
        locations: Set[str] = set()
        if not os.path.exists(file_path):
            return locations
        with open(file_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f_in:
            for raw_line in f_in:
                cleaned: str = raw_line.strip()
                if not cleaned or cleaned.startswith("#"):
                    continue
                for item in cleaned.split(","):
                    norm: str = cls.normalize_token(item)
                    if len(norm) >= 3:
                        locations.add(norm)
        return locations

    @classmethod
    def load_words_set(cls, file_path: str) -> Set[str]:
        """
        Charge une liste de mots exclus depuis un fichier texte
        (un mot par ligne ou séparé par des virgules, commentaires '#' ignorés).

        :param file_path: Chemin vers mots_exclus.txt.
        :return: Ensemble des mots normalisés sans accents.
        """
        words: Set[str] = set()
        if not os.path.exists(file_path):
            return words
        with open(file_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f_in:
            for raw_line in f_in:
                cleaned: str = raw_line.strip()
                if not cleaned or cleaned.startswith("#"):
                    continue
                for item in cleaned.split(","):
                    norm: str = cls.normalize_token(item)
                    if norm:
                        words.add(norm)
        return words

FICHIER : doc_anonymizer/__init__.py
================================================================================
# Commandes pip à exécuter : Aucune dépendance externe requise

"""
Package dédié à l'extraction multi-formats et à l'anonymisation locale de documents.
"""

================================================================================
FICHIER : doc_anonymizer/anonymization_runner.py
================================================================================
# Commandes pip à exécuter : Aucune dépendance externe requise

import os
import re
import time
from typing import List, Pattern
from doc_anonymizer.audit_state_manager import AuditAndStateManager
from doc_anonymizer.comm_extractors import CommunicationExtractor
from doc_anonymizer.config_manager import PipelineConfig
from doc_anonymizer.contact_rules_loader import ContactRulesLoader
from doc_anonymizer.text_utils import DocumentTextUtils


class DocumentAnonymizationRunner:
    """
    Orchestre l'anonymisation par lots au fil de l'eau et l'audit
    en temps réel des noms propres résiduels.
    """

    TEXT_CHUNK_LIMIT: int = 32768

    AUTO_TITLE_PATTERN: Pattern[str] = re.compile(
        r"(?<![A-Za-zÀ-ÿ])(Dr|Docteur|DOCTEUR|Pr|Professeur|Mme|MME|Madame|M\.|Monsieur|"
        r"Mlle|Mademoiselle|Me|Maître|Maitre|Famille|Pharmacie|PHARMACIE)\.?\s+"
        r"(?:((?:de|du|des|d'|le|la|van|von|ben|el|al|ould|dos|da|di)(?:\s+(?:la|le|les))?)\s+)?"
        r"([A-ZÉÈÊËÀÂÄÎÏÔÖÙÛÜÇ]"
        r"[A-Za-zÉÈÊËÀÂÄÎÏÔÖÙÛÜÇéèêëàâäîïôöùûüç'-]{2,25}(?:\s+[A-ZÉÈÊËÀÂÄÎÏÔÖÙÛÜÇ][A-Za-zÉÈÊËÀÂÄÎÏÔÖÙÛÜÇéèêëàâäîïôöùûüç'-]{2,25})?)(?![A-Za-zÀ-ÿ])"
    )

    AUTO_PERSON_PAIR_PATTERN: Pattern[str] = re.compile(
        r"(?<![A-Za-zÀ-ÿ])([A-Za-zÉÈÊËÀÂÄÎÏÔÖÙÛÜÇéèêëàâäîïôöùûüç'-]{3,22})\s+"
        r"(?:((?:De|Du|Des|de|du|des|Le|La|le|la|Van|Von|Ben|El|Al|Ould|Dos|Da|Di)(?:\s+(?:La|Le|Les|la|le|les))?)\s+)?"
        r"([A-ZÉÈÊËÀÂÄÎÏÔÖÙÛÜÇ]"
        r"[A-Za-zÉÈÊËÀÂÄÎÏÔÖÙÛÜÇéèêëàâäîïôöùûüç'-]{2,25}(?:\s+[A-ZÉÈÊËÀÂÄÎÏÔÖÙÛÜÇ][A-Za-zÉÈÊËÀÂÄÎÏÔÖÙÛÜÇéèêëàâäîïôöùûüç'-]{2,25})?)(?![A-Za-zÀ-ÿ])"
    )

    UPPER_PAIR_PATTERN: Pattern[str] = re.compile(
        r"(?<![A-Za-zÀ-ÿ])([A-ZÉÈÊËÀÂÄÎÏÔÖÙÛÜÇ][a-zéèêëàâäîïôöùûüç'-]{2,22})\s+([A-ZÉÈÊËÀÂÄÎÏÔÖÙÛÜÇ]{4,25})(?![A-Za-zÀ-ÿ])"
        r"|(?<![A-Za-zÀ-ÿ])([A-ZÉÈÊËÀÂÄÎÏÔÖÙÛÜÇ]{4,25})\s+([A-ZÉÈÊËÀÂÄÎÏÔÖÙÛÜÇ][a-zéèêëàâäîïôöùûüç'-]{2,22})(?![A-Za-zÀ-ÿ])"
    )

    GLUED_TITLE_OR_CAMEL: Pattern[str] = re.compile(
        r"\b(DOCTEUR|Docteur|Dr|DR|MME|Mme|MADAME|MONSIEUR|Monsieur|PROFESSEUR|PHARMACIE|CHU|Enfant|IDFR[A-Z]?)"
        r"([A-ZÉÈÊËÀÂÄÎÏÔÖÙÛÜÇ][A-Za-zÉÈÊËÀÂÄÎÏÔÖÙÛÜÇéèêëàâäîïôöùûüç]{3,})\b"
        r"|([a-zà-ÿ]{3,})([A-ZÉÈÊËÀÂÄÎÏÔÖÙÛÜÇ]{4,})\b"
        r"|([A-ZÉÈÊËÀÂÄÎÏÔÖÙÛÜÇ]{4,})([A-ZÉÈÊËÀÂÄÎÏÔÖÙÛÜÇ][a-zà-ÿ]{3,})\b"
    )

    DUPLICATE_ALIAS_CLEANUP: Pattern[str] = re.compile(
        r"\b(Docteur|Dr|Mme|M\.|Pr|Me)(?:\s+\1)+\b"
        r"|\b([A-Z]\.(?:-[A-Z]\.)?)(?:\s+\2)+\b"
    )

    def __init__(
        self, config: PipelineConfig, manual_only: bool = False
    ) -> None:
        """Initialise l'orchestrateur d'anonymisation."""
        self.config: PipelineConfig = config
        self.manual_only: bool = manual_only
        self.rules: ContactRulesLoader = ContactRulesLoader(
            config, manual_only=manual_only
        )
        self.audit: AuditAndStateManager = AuditAndStateManager(
            config, self.rules
        )
        self._initialized: bool = False
        self.current_chunk: List[str] = []
        self.current_size: int = 0
        self.current_batch_files: List[str] = []
        self.processed_in_session: int = 0

    def initialize(self) -> bool:
        """Charge les règles et restaure l'état JSON de reprise."""
        if self._initialized:
            return True
        if not self.rules.load_all():
            print(
                "[ERREUR ANONYMIZER] Chargement des règles impossible.",
                flush=True,
            )
            return False
        self.audit.load()
        print(
            f"[INIT ANONYMIZER] État chargé : "
            f"{len(self.audit.already_processed)} fichier(s) déjà traité(s), "
            f"prochain index de lot = {self.audit.file_idx}.",
            flush=True,
        )
        self._initialized = True
        return True
    @staticmethod
    def _split_glued_tokens(match: re.Match[str]) -> str:
        """Sépare les jetons collés par casse CamelCase ou titre majuscule."""
        if match.group(1) and match.group(2):
            return f"{match.group(1)} {match.group(2)}"
        if match.group(3) and match.group(4):
            return f"{match.group(3)} {match.group(4)}"
        return f"{match.group(5)} {match.group(6)}"

    def _is_non_surname_word(self, word: str) -> bool:
        """Vérifie si un mot est un mot-outil, un lieu ou un terme exclu."""
        for tok in word.split():
            norm: str = DocumentTextUtils.normalize_word(tok)
            if (
                norm in AuditAndStateManager.COMMON_FRENCH_STOPWORDS
                or norm in self.config.excluded_words
                or norm in self.config.masked_locations
                or norm in self.rules.alias_words
                or norm in DocumentTextUtils.ORG_GENERIC_WORDS
            ):
                return True
        return False

    @staticmethod
    def _format_surname_initials(surname: str) -> str:
        """Formate un nom de famille en initiale(s) majuscule(s)."""
        space_parts: List[str] = [p.strip() for p in surname.split() if p.strip()]
        if len(space_parts) >= 2:
            return " ".join(
                DocumentAnonymizationRunner._format_surname_initials(sp)
                for sp in space_parts
            )
        if "-" in surname:
            parts: List[str] = [p.strip() for p in surname.split("-") if p.strip()]
            if len(parts) >= 2:
                return "-".join(f"{p[:1].upper()}." for p in parts)
        return f"{surname.strip()[:1].upper()}."

    @staticmethod
    def _format_particle_initials(particle: str) -> str:
        """Abrège une particule nobiliaire par son initiale."""
        if not particle or not particle.strip():
            return ""
        tokens: List[str] = [t for t in particle.strip().split() if t]
        return " ".join(f"{t[:1].upper()}." for t in tokens) + " "

    def _is_known_first_name_ocr(self, token: str) -> bool:
        """Vérifie si le jeton est un prénom connu (avec tolérance OCR 'l' -> 'I')."""
        if self.rules.is_first_name(token):
            return True
        if token.startswith("l") and len(token) >= 5:
            return self.rules.is_first_name("I" + token[1:])
        return False

    @staticmethod
    def _is_title_case_token(token: str) -> bool:
        """Indique si un jeton commence par une majuscule suivie de minuscules."""
        return (
            len(token) >= 3
            and token[:1].isupper()
            and token[1:].islower()
        )

    def _replace_title_match(self, match: re.Match[str]) -> str:
        """Remplace un nom précédé d'une civilité ou d'un titre."""
        title: str = match.group(1)
        particle: str = match.group(2) or ""
        surname: str = match.group(3)
        if self._is_non_surname_word(surname):
            return match.group(0)

        norm_sur: str = DocumentTextUtils.normalize_word(surname)
        if not particle:
            known_aliases = self.rules.last_name_to_aliases.get(norm_sur, set())
            if len(known_aliases) == 1:
                return next(iter(known_aliases))

        is_protected: bool = norm_sur in self.rules.protected_surnames
        if (
            not is_protected
            and self._is_known_first_name_ocr(surname)
            and title.lower() not in {
                "dr", "docteur", "pr", "professeur", "me", "maître", "maitre",
            }
        ):
            return match.group(0)

        part_init: str = self._format_particle_initials(particle)
        sur_init: str = self._format_surname_initials(surname)
        return f"{title} {part_init}{sur_init}"
    def _replace_upper_pair_match(self, match: re.Match[str]) -> str:
        """Masque les paires Prénom NOM et NOM Prénom sans collision avec les mots-outils."""
        if match.group(1) and match.group(2):
            first_n, last_n = match.group(1), match.group(2)
            if self._is_non_surname_word(first_n) or self._is_non_surname_word(last_n):
                return match.group(0)
            return f"{first_n} {self._format_surname_initials(last_n)}"
        last_n, first_n = match.group(3), match.group(4)
        if self._is_non_surname_word(last_n) or self._is_non_surname_word(first_n):
            return match.group(0)
        return f"{self._format_surname_initials(last_n)} {first_n}"

    def _replace_person_pair_match(self, match: re.Match[str]) -> str:
        """Remplace une paire Prénom Nom ou NOM Prénom détectée en contexte."""
        w1: str = match.group(1)
        particle: str = match.group(2) or ""
        w2: str = match.group(3)
        if self._is_non_surname_word(w1) or self._is_non_surname_word(w2):
            return match.group(0)

        f1: bool = self._is_known_first_name_ocr(w1)
        w2_parts: List[str] = [p for p in w2.split() if p]
        f2: bool = len(w2_parts) == 1 and self._is_known_first_name_ocr(w2)
        part_init: str = self._format_particle_initials(particle)

        if w2.isupper() and len(w2) >= 4 and (f1 or self._is_title_case_token(w1)):
            return f"{w1} {part_init}{self._format_surname_initials(w2)}"

        if w1.isupper() and len(w1) >= 4 and len(w2_parts) == 1 and (f2 or self._is_title_case_token(w2)):
            return f"{self._format_surname_initials(w1)} {part_init}{w2}"

        if f1 and (not f2 or particle or len(w2_parts) >= 2) and len(w2) >= 3:
            return f"{w1} {part_init}{self._format_surname_initials(w2)}"

        if not f1 and f2 and len(w1) >= 3:
            if w1.isupper() or DocumentTextUtils.normalize_word(w1) in self.rules.protected_surnames:
                return f"{self._format_surname_initials(w1)} {part_init}{w2}"

        return match.group(0)

    def _anonymize_chunk(self, text: str) -> str:
        """Applique les passes de masquage sur une tranche de texte bornée."""
        token: str = CommunicationExtractor.MAIN_CHARACTER_TOKEN
        text = text.replace(token, self.config.main_character_name)
        text = DocumentTextUtils.repair_mojibake(text)
        text = self.GLUED_TITLE_OR_CAMEL.sub(self._split_glued_tokens, text)

        if self.config.mask_ssn:
            text = DocumentTextUtils.REGEX_SSN.sub("[NUM_SECU_MASQUE]", text)
        if self.config.mask_financial_and_admin_ids:
            text = DocumentTextUtils.REGEX_IBAN.sub("[IBAN_MASQUE]", text)
            text = DocumentTextUtils.REGEX_BIC.sub("[BIC_MASQUE]", text)
            text = DocumentTextUtils.REGEX_FISCAL_ID.sub("[NUM_FISCAL_MASQUE]", text)
            text = DocumentTextUtils.REGEX_FRANCE_TRAVAIL.sub("[ID_FRANCE_TRAVAIL_MASQUE]", text)
            text = DocumentTextUtils.REGEX_CAF_ALLOCATAIRE.sub("[NUM_ALLOCATAIRE_MASQUE]", text)
        text = DocumentTextUtils.REGEX_EMAIL.sub(
            lambda m: self.audit.pseudonymize_id(m.group(0), "EMAIL"), text
        )
        text = DocumentTextUtils.REGEX_PHONE.sub(
            lambda m: self.audit.pseudonymize_id(m.group(0), "TEL"), text
        )
        if self.config.mask_urls:
            text = DocumentTextUtils.REGEX_URL.sub("[URL_MASQUEE]", text)

        text = text.replace("//", " ")

        if self.config.mask_addresses:
            text = DocumentTextUtils.REGEX_POSTAL_CITY.sub("[LIEU_MASQUE]", text)
            text = DocumentTextUtils.REGEX_STREET_ADDRESS.sub("[ADRESSE_MASQUEE]", text)

        if self.rules.combined_glued_pattern is not None:
            text = self.rules.combined_glued_pattern.sub(
                lambda m: self.rules.lookup_glued_alias(m.group(0)), text
            )

        text = self.AUTO_TITLE_PATTERN.sub(self._replace_title_match, text)

        if self.rules.combined_keyword_pattern is not None:
            text = self.rules.combined_keyword_pattern.sub(
                lambda m: self.rules.lookup_keyword_alias(m.group(0)), text
            )

        text = self.UPPER_PAIR_PATTERN.sub(self._replace_upper_pair_match, text)
        text = self.AUTO_PERSON_PAIR_PATTERN.sub(self._replace_person_pair_match, text)

        if self.rules.combined_location_pattern is not None:
            text = self.rules.combined_location_pattern.sub("[LIEU_MASQUE]", text)

        text = self.DUPLICATE_ALIAS_CLEANUP.sub(
            lambda m: m.group(1) or m.group(2), text
        )
        return text

    def _anonymize_text(self, text: str) -> str:
        """Découpe les textes volumineux par tranches de 32 Ko pour éviter tout blocage regex."""
        if len(text) <= self.TEXT_CHUNK_LIMIT:
            return self._anonymize_chunk(text)
        out_parts: List[str] = []
        buf: List[str] = []
        buf_len: int = 0
        for line in text.splitlines(keepends=True):
            buf.append(line)
            buf_len += len(line)
            if buf_len >= self.TEXT_CHUNK_LIMIT:
                out_parts.append(self._anonymize_chunk("".join(buf)))
                buf = []
                buf_len = 0
        if buf:
            out_parts.append(self._anonymize_chunk("".join(buf)))
        return "".join(out_parts)
    def _anonymize_display_path(self, trans_path: str) -> str:
        """Masque les identifiants sensibles dans le nom de fichier source affiché."""
        folder, file_name = os.path.split(trans_path)
        masked: str = DocumentTextUtils.repair_mojibake(file_name)
        if self.config.mask_ssn:
            masked = DocumentTextUtils.REGEX_SSN.sub("NUM_SECU_MASQUE", masked)
        if self.config.mask_financial_and_admin_ids:
            masked = DocumentTextUtils.REGEX_IBAN.sub("IBAN_MASQUE", masked)
        masked = DocumentTextUtils.REGEX_EMAIL.sub(
            lambda m: self.audit.pseudonymize_id(m.group(0), "EMAIL"), masked
        )
        masked = DocumentTextUtils.REGEX_PHONE.sub(
            lambda m: self.audit.pseudonymize_id(m.group(0), "TEL"), masked
        )
        if self.config.mask_urls:
            masked = DocumentTextUtils.REGEX_URL.sub("URL_MASQUEE", masked)
        masked = self.AUTO_TITLE_PATTERN.sub(self._replace_title_match, masked)
        if self.rules.combined_keyword_pattern is not None:
            masked = self.rules.combined_keyword_pattern.sub(
                lambda m: self.rules.lookup_keyword_alias(m.group(0)), masked
            )
        if self.rules.combined_glued_pattern is not None:
            masked = self.rules.combined_glued_pattern.sub(
                lambda m: self.rules.lookup_glued_alias(m.group(0)), masked
            )
        masked = self.AUTO_PERSON_PAIR_PATTERN.sub(self._replace_person_pair_match, masked)
        if self.rules.combined_location_pattern is not None:
            masked = self.rules.combined_location_pattern.sub("LIEU_MASQUE", masked)
        return os.path.join(folder, masked)

    def flush_batch(self) -> None:
        """Écrit le lot en cours sur disque et met à jour l'état et l'audit."""
        if not self.current_chunk:
            self.audit.save()
            self.audit.write_report()
            return
        batch_num: int = ((self.audit.file_idx - 1) // self.config.files_per_folder) + 1
        batch_dir: str = os.path.join(self.config.results_dir, f"lot_{batch_num:02d}")
        os.makedirs(batch_dir, exist_ok=True)
        out_name: str = f"{self.config.file_prefix}partie_{self.audit.file_idx:03d}.txt"
        full_out_path: str = os.path.join(batch_dir, out_name)
        DocumentTextUtils.atomic_write_text(full_out_path, "".join(self.current_chunk))

        self.audit.already_processed.update(self.current_batch_files)
        self.audit.file_idx += 1
        self.audit.written_batches += 1
        self.current_chunk = []
        self.current_size = 0
        self.current_batch_files = []

        self.audit.save()
        count: int = self.audit.write_report()
        print(f"    [+] LOT ÉCRIT : '{full_out_path}' (Audit : {count} nom(s))", flush=True)

    def anonymize_single_file(self, trans_path: str, force_reprocess: bool = False) -> bool:
        """Anonymise une transcription individuelle et l'ajoute au lot courant."""
        if not self._initialized and not self.initialize():
            return False
        norm_path: str = os.path.normpath(trans_path)
        if not force_reprocess and norm_path in self.audit.already_processed:
            return False
        if not os.path.exists(trans_path):
            return False

        with open(trans_path, "r", encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore") as f:
            raw_content: str = f.read()
        if not raw_content.strip():
            return False

        display_path: str = self._anonymize_display_path(trans_path)
        sep: str = "#" * 72
        header: str = f"{sep}\n# SOURCE TRANSCRIPTION : {display_path}\n{sep}\n"
        anonymized: str = self._anonymize_text(header + raw_content)
        self.audit.scan_residual_names(anonymized, display_path)

        block_bytes: int = len(anonymized.encode("utf-8"))
        max_files_per_batch: int = getattr(self.config, "max_batch_files", 50)
        if self.current_chunk and (
            self.current_size + block_bytes > self.config.max_batch_bytes
            or len(self.current_chunk) >= max_files_per_batch
        ):
            self.flush_batch()

        self.current_chunk.append(anonymized)
        self.current_size += block_bytes
        self.current_batch_files.append(norm_path)
        self.processed_in_session += 1
        return True
    def finalize(self, start_time: float) -> None:
        """Vide le dernier lot et génère les rapports finaux d'audit."""
        self.flush_batch()
        count: int = self.audit.write_report()
        elapsed: float = time.time() - start_time
        print(f"\n[100.0%] Terminé ({self.processed_in_session} fichier(s) anonymisé(s) en {elapsed:.1f}s).", flush=True)
        print(f" * Lots totaux  : {self.audit.written_batches} dans '{self.config.results_dir}/lot_XX/'", flush=True)
        print(f" * Audit détail : {count} nom(s) dans '{self.config.audit_report_file}'", flush=True)
        print(f" * Audit copier : {count} nom(s) dans '{self.config.audit_copy_file}'", flush=True)

    def run_on_existing_results(self) -> None:
        """Réapplique les règles de contacts sur les lots déjà générés."""
        start_time: float = time.time()
        print("=== PASSE SUR PLACE : CONTACTS & AUTOMATISMES SUR RÉSULTATS ===", flush=True)
        if not self.initialize():
            return
        res_dir: str = self.config.results_dir
        if not os.path.isdir(res_dir):
            print(f"Erreur : '{res_dir}' introuvable.", flush=True)
            return

        lot_files: List[str] = sorted(
            os.path.join(rp, fn)
            for rp, _, files in os.walk(res_dir)
            for fn in files
            if fn.lower().endswith(".txt") and not fn.startswith(".")
        )
        if not lot_files:
            print(f"Aucun fichier de lot trouvé dans '{res_dir}'.", flush=True)
            return

        self.audit.pair_counter.clear()
        self.audit.files_by_name.clear()
        self.audit.canonical_labels.clear()

        total: int = len(lot_files)
        for idx, lot_path in enumerate(lot_files, start=1):
            with open(lot_path, "r", encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore") as f_in:
                updated_content: str = self._anonymize_text(f_in.read())
            DocumentTextUtils.atomic_write_text(lot_path, updated_content)
            self.audit.scan_residual_names(updated_content, os.path.basename(lot_path))
            if idx % 10 == 0 or idx == total or idx == 1:
                print(f"    ... [{idx}/{total}] {os.path.basename(lot_path)}", flush=True)

        self.audit.save()
        count: int = self.audit.write_report()
        print(f"\n[100.0%] Passe sur place terminée sur {total} lot(s) en {time.time() - start_time:.1f}s.", flush=True)
        print(f" * Audit détail : {count} nom(s) dans '{self.config.audit_report_file}'", flush=True)
        print(f" * Audit copier : {count} nom(s) dans '{self.config.audit_copy_file}'", flush=True)

    def run(self) -> None:
        """Exécute l'anonymisation complète sur le répertoire transcriptions."""
        if self.manual_only:
            self.run_on_existing_results()
            return
        start_time: float = time.time()
        print("=== ÉTAPE 2 : ANONYMISATION & AUDIT ===", flush=True)
        if not self.initialize():
            return
        trans_dir: str = self.config.transcriptions_dir
        if not os.path.exists(trans_dir):
            print(f"Erreur : '{trans_dir}' introuvable.", flush=True)
            return

        all_txts: List[str] = sorted(
            os.path.join(rp, fn)
            for rp, _, files in os.walk(trans_dir)
            for fn in files
            if fn.lower().endswith(".txt") and fn != "rapport_extraction.txt" and not fn.startswith(".")
        )
        pending: List[str] = [f for f in all_txts if os.path.normpath(f) not in self.audit.already_processed]
        total: int = len(pending)
        for idx, trans_path in enumerate(pending, start=1):
            if idx % 10 == 0 or idx == total or idx == 1:
                print(f"    ... [{(idx / total) * 100:5.1f}% | {idx}/{total}] ...", flush=True)
            self.anonymize_single_file(trans_path)
        self.finalize(start_time)

================================================================================
FICHIER : doc_anonymizer/audit_state_manager.py
================================================================================
# Commandes pip à exécuter : Aucune dépendance externe requise

from collections import Counter, defaultdict
from difflib import SequenceMatcher
import hashlib
import json
import os
import re
from typing import Any, DefaultDict, Dict, FrozenSet, List, Optional, Pattern, Set, Tuple
from doc_anonymizer.config_manager import PipelineConfig
from doc_anonymizer.contact_rules_loader import ContactRulesLoader
from doc_anonymizer.text_utils import DocumentTextUtils


class AuditAndStateManager:
    """
    Gère la persistance JSON de reprise, la pseudonymisation SHA-256
    et la génération des rapports d'audit avec filtrage strict des faux positifs
    et rapprochement orthographique/phonétique.
    """

    PAIR_PATTERN: Pattern[str] = re.compile(
        r"\b([A-ZÉÈÊËÀÂÄÎÏÔÖÙÛÜÇ]"
        r"[A-Za-zÉÈÊËÀÂÄÎÏÔÖÙÛÜÇéèêëàâäîïôöùûüç-]{1,25})[ \t]+"
        r"(?:(?:de|du|des|d'|le|la|van|von|ben|el|al|dos|da|di)[ \t]+)?"
        r"([A-ZÉÈÊËÀÂÄÎÏÔÖÙÛÜÇ]"
        r"[A-Za-zÉÈÊËÀÂÄÎÏÔÖÙÛÜÇéèêëàâäîïôöùûüç-]{1,25})\b"
    )

    TITLE_PATTERN: Pattern[str] = re.compile(
        r"\b(?:Dr|Docteur|Pr|Professeur|Mme|Madame|M\.|Monsieur|"
        r"Mlle|Mademoiselle|Me|Maître|Maitre|Famille)\.?\s+"
        r"(?:(?:de|du|des|d'|le|la|van|von|ben|el|al|dos|da|di)\s+)?"
        r"([A-ZÉÈÊËÀÂÄÎÏÔÖÙÛÜÇ]"
        r"[A-Za-zÉÈÊËÀÂÄÎÏÔÖÙÛÜÇéèêëàâäîïôöùûüç-]{1,25})\b"
    )

    MASKED_PREFIXES: Tuple[str, ...] = (
        "TEL_", "EMAIL_", "AUTRE_", "CONTACT_MAIL_",
        "LIEU_MASQUE", "URL_MASQUEE", "ADRESSE_MASQUEE",
        "NUM_SECU", "IBAN_", "BIC_", "ID_FRANCE", "NUM_ALLOCATAIRE",
        "NUM_FISCAL", "PERSONNAGE_",
    )

    COMMON_FRENCH_STOPWORDS: FrozenSet[str] = frozenset({
        "le", "la", "les", "un", "une", "des", "du", "de", "au", "aux",
        "ce", "cet", "cette", "ces", "mon", "ton", "son", "ma", "ta", "sa",
        "mes", "tes", "ses", "notre", "votre", "leur", "nos", "vos", "leurs",
        "je", "tu", "il", "elle", "on", "nous", "vous", "ils", "elles",
        "qui", "que", "quoi", "dont", "ou", "et", "mais", "donc", "or", "ni",
        "car", "pour", "par", "avec", "sans", "sous", "sur", "dans", "vers",
        "chez", "entre", "apres", "avant", "depuis", "pendant", "selon",
        "tres", "plus", "moins", "bien", "mal", "tout", "tous", "toute",
        "toutes", "autre", "autres", "meme", "memes", "aucun", "aucune",
        "chaque", "quel", "quelle", "quels", "quelles", "ici", "la", "oui",
        "non", "pas", "ne", "en", "y", "est", "sont", "etait", "etaient",
        "etre", "avoir", "fait", "faire", "dit", "dire", "voir", "vu",
        "bonjour", "bonsoir", "salut", "merci", "cordialement", "sinceres",
        "salutations", "objet", "sujet", "date", "heure", "lieu", "page",
        "pages", "total", "montant", "numero", "reference", "dossier",
        "document", "documents", "fichier", "fichiers", "piece", "jointe",
        "annexe", "copie", "original", "version", "note", "notes", "avis",
        "compte", "rendu", "bilan", "rapport", "synthese", "resume",
        "contrat", "facture", "devis", "commande", "paiement", "reglement",
        "attestation", "certificat", "ordonnance", "prescription", "courrier",
        "lettre", "message", "mail", "email", "telephone", "adresse", "code",
        "postal", "ville", "pays", "france", "europe", "region", "departement",
        "mairie", "prefecture", "tribunal", "ministere", "service", "services",
        "direction", "directeur", "directrice", "president", "presidente",
        "general", "generale", "secretaire", "responsable", "chef", "agent",
        "docteur", "professeur", "madame", "monsieur", "mademoiselle", "maitre",
        "cabinet", "clinique", "hopital", "centre", "laboratoire", "pharmacie",
        "banque", "assurance", "mutuelle", "caisse", "allocations", "impots",
        "article", "chapitre", "section", "titre", "point", "liste", "tableau",
        "lundi", "mardi", "mercredi", "jeudi", "vendredi", "samedi", "dimanche",
        "janvier", "fevrier", "mars", "avril", "mai", "juin", "juillet",
        "aout", "septembre", "octobre", "novembre", "decembre", "janv", "fevr",
        "avr", "juil", "sept", "oct", "nov", "dec", "nord", "sud", "est",
        "ouest", "saint", "sainte", "rue", "avenue", "boulevard", "place",
    })
    AMBIGUOUS_FIRST_NAMES: FrozenSet[str] = frozenset({
        "aime", "aimee", "ange", "aube", "aurore", "blanc", "blanche",
        "bon", "cerise", "clair", "claire", "clement", "constance", "desire",
        "douce", "esperance", "fidele", "fleur", "flore", "france", "gai",
        "grace", "guy", "honore", "iris", "jean", "joie", "joli", "joy",
        "juste", "laurier", "loup", "luce", "lumiere", "marc", "marine",
        "max", "may", "modeste", "noel", "olive", "paix", "parfait",
        "patience", "pierre", "premier", "prime", "prudence", "rose",
        "sage", "sauveur", "solange", "soleil", "victoire", "violette",
    })

    def __init__(
        self, config: PipelineConfig, rules: ContactRulesLoader
    ) -> None:
        """
        Initialise le gestionnaire d'état et les compteurs d'audit.

        :param config: Instance de PipelineConfig.
        :param rules: Instance de ContactRulesLoader.
        """
        self.config: PipelineConfig = config
        self.rules: ContactRulesLoader = rules
        self.already_processed: Set[str] = set()
        self.auto_cache: Dict[str, str] = {}
        self.counters: Dict[str, int] = {"TEL": 1, "EMAIL": 1}
        self.pair_counter: Counter[str] = Counter()
        self.files_by_name: DefaultDict[str, Set[str]] = defaultdict(set)
        self.canonical_labels: Dict[str, str] = {}
        self.file_idx: int = 1
        self.written_batches: int = 0

    @staticmethod
    def _phonetic_french_key(word: str) -> str:
        """
        Calcule une clé phonétique française simplifiée pour rapprocher les
        erreurs de transcription vocale/OCR (ex: 'dubunt' ~ 'dupont').

        :param word: Mot normalisé sans accents.
        :return: Empreinte phonétique compacte.
        """
        s: str = DocumentTextUtils.normalize_word(word)
        s = re.sub(r"[^a-z]", "", s)
        if not s:
            return ""
        s = s.replace("ph", "f").replace("qu", "k").replace("q", "k")
        s = s.replace("c", "k").replace("g", "k")
        s = s.replace("p", "b").replace("t", "d")
        s = s.replace("v", "f").replace("z", "s")
        s = re.sub(r"[aeiouy]+", "a", s)
        s = re.sub(r"(.)\1+", r"\1", s)
        if len(s) > 3 and s[-1] in {"d", "s", "x"}:
            s = s[:-1]
        return s

    def _suggest_alias_from_fuzzy_last_name(
        self, display_name: str
    ) -> Optional[Tuple[str, str]]:
        """
        Recherche si le nom candidat (ou son nom de famille) est proche
        orthographiquement ou phonétiquement d'un nom connu dans contacts.

        :param display_name: Libellé candidat détecté par l'audit.
        :return: Tuple (alias_suggéré, nom_famille_proche) ou None.
        """
        if not self.rules.last_name_to_aliases:
            return None

        tokens: List[str] = display_name.strip().split()
        candidate_last: str = tokens[-1] if tokens else display_name.strip()
        norm_cand: str = DocumentTextUtils.normalize_word(candidate_last)
        if len(norm_cand) < 4:
            return None

        cand_phon: str = self._phonetic_french_key(norm_cand)
        best_score: float = 0.0
        best_known: str = ""

        for known_last in self.rules.last_name_to_aliases.keys():
            if len(known_last) < 4 or known_last == norm_cand:
                continue
            ratio: float = SequenceMatcher(
                None, norm_cand, known_last
            ).ratio()
            known_phon: str = self._phonetic_french_key(known_last)
            if cand_phon and cand_phon == known_phon:
                ratio = max(ratio, 0.85)
            if ratio > best_score:
                best_score = ratio
                best_known = known_last

        if best_score >= 0.72 and best_known:
            aliases: List[str] = sorted(
                self.rules.last_name_to_aliases[best_known]
            )
            if len(aliases) == 1:
                disp_known: str = self.rules.last_name_display.get(
                    best_known, best_known.capitalize()
                )
                return next(iter(aliases)), disp_known
        return None
    @staticmethod
    def _format_first_name_case(token: str) -> str:
        """
        Capitalise proprement un prénom (gère les prénoms composés et majuscules).

        :param token: Jeton prénom brut.
        :return: Prénom mis en forme (ex: 'FLORIAN' -> 'Florian').
        """
        parts: List[str] = re.split(r"(-)", token.strip())
        return "".join(p.capitalize() if p != "-" else p for p in parts)

    def _canonicalize_key(self, raw_key: str) -> Tuple[str, str]:
        """
        Ordonne une paire en 'Prénom Nom' grâce au référentiel des prénoms
        et retourne (clé_normalisée_unique, libellé_affichable).

        :param raw_key: Clé candidate ('Nom Prénom', 'Prénom Nom' ou nom seul).
        :return: Tuple (identifiant_canonique, libellé_d_affichage).
        """
        tokens: List[str] = raw_key.strip().split()
        if len(tokens) == 2:
            p1, p2 = tokens
            f1: bool = self.rules.is_first_name(p1)
            f2: bool = self.rules.is_first_name(p2)
            if f2 and not f1:
                first_tok, last_tok = p2, p1
            else:
                first_tok, last_tok = p1, p2
            display_first: str = self._format_first_name_case(first_tok)
            display_label: str = f"{display_first} {last_tok}"
        else:
            display_label = raw_key.strip()

        norm_id: str = DocumentTextUtils.normalize_word(display_label)
        existing: str = self.canonical_labels.get(norm_id, "")
        if not existing or (existing.isupper() and not display_label.isupper()):
            self.canonical_labels[norm_id] = display_label
        return norm_id, self.canonical_labels[norm_id]

    def _is_valid_word(self, word: str) -> bool:
        """
        Vérifie qu'un mot candidat est un nom propre plausible et non un
        jeton masqué, un mot-outil français ou un terme exclu.

        :param word: Mot brut à évaluer.
        :return: True si le mot est éligible pour l'audit.
        """
        cleaned: str = word.strip()
        if len(cleaned) < 2 or cleaned.upper().startswith(self.MASKED_PREFIXES):
            return False
        if any(ch.isdigit() for ch in cleaned):
            return False
        norm: str = DocumentTextUtils.normalize_word(cleaned)
        if len(norm) < 2:
            return False
        return (
            norm not in self.COMMON_FRENCH_STOPWORDS
            and norm not in self.config.excluded_words
            and norm not in self.rules.alias_words
            and norm not in self.config.masked_locations
        )

    def _is_plausible_pair(self, p1: str, p2: str) -> bool:
        """
        Valide une paire (p1, p2) en écartant les faux positifs liés aux
        prénoms ambigus (mots courants) et aux sigles tout en majuscules.

        :param p1: Premier mot candidat.
        :param p2: Second mot candidat.
        :return: True si la paire est un nom de personne probable.
        """
        if not (self._is_valid_word(p1) and self._is_valid_word(p2)):
            return False
        if p1.isupper() and p2.isupper() and len(p1) <= 4 and len(p2) <= 4:
            return False

        f1: bool = self.rules.is_first_name(p1)
        f2: bool = self.rules.is_first_name(p2)
        if not (f1 or f2) or (f1 and f2):
            return False

        first_tok: str = p1 if f1 else p2
        last_tok: str = p2 if f1 else p1
        norm_first: str = DocumentTextUtils.normalize_word(first_tok)
        norm_last: str = DocumentTextUtils.normalize_word(last_tok)

        if len(norm_last) < 3:
            return False
        if norm_first in self.AMBIGUOUS_FIRST_NAMES:
            if (
                norm_last not in self.rules.last_name_to_aliases
                and not last_tok.isupper()
            ):
                return False

        nk: str = DocumentTextUtils.normalize_word(f"{p1} {p2}")
        ni: str = DocumentTextUtils.normalize_word(f"{p2} {p1}")
        return (
            nk not in self.rules.full_targets
            and ni not in self.rules.full_targets
        )
    def _is_entry_still_valid(self, display_name: str) -> bool:
        """
        Vérifie si une entrée déjà présente dans l'état JSON reste valide
        après application des nouveaux filtres anti-faux positifs.

        :param display_name: Libellé enregistré dans l'état.
        :return: True si l'entrée doit être conservée.
        """
        tokens: List[str] = display_name.strip().split()
        if len(tokens) == 2:
            tok_first, tok_second = tokens
            return self._is_plausible_pair(tok_first, tok_second)
        if len(tokens) == 1:
            tok: str = next(iter(tokens))
            norm: str = DocumentTextUtils.normalize_word(tok)
            return (
                self._is_valid_word(tok)
                and len(norm) >= 3
                and norm not in self.rules.full_targets
            )
        return False

    def load(self) -> None:
        """Restaure, filtre et fusionne l'état JSON depuis le disque s'il existe."""
        os.makedirs(self.config.results_dir, exist_ok=True)
        if not os.path.exists(self.config.resume_state_file):
            return
        try:
            with open(
                self.config.resume_state_file, "r", encoding="utf-8"
            ) as f:
                st: Dict[str, Any] = json.load(f)
            self.already_processed = set(st.get("fichiers_traites", []))
            self.auto_cache = st.get("cache_auto", {})
            self.counters = st.get("compteurs", {"TEL": 1, "EMAIL": 1})
            self.file_idx = st.get("idx_fichier", 1)
            self.written_batches = st.get("nb_lots_ecrits", 0)

            raw_counts: Dict[str, int] = st.get("compteur_paires", {})
            raw_files: Dict[str, List[str]] = st.get("fichiers_par_nom", {})
            for raw_name, count in raw_counts.items():
                if not self._is_entry_still_valid(raw_name):
                    continue
                norm_id, _ = self._canonicalize_key(raw_name)
                self.pair_counter[norm_id] += int(count)
                self.files_by_name[norm_id].update(raw_files.get(raw_name, []))
        except Exception:
            pass

    def save(self) -> None:
        """Sauvegarde l'état courant sur le disque de façon atomique."""
        os.makedirs(self.config.results_dir, exist_ok=True)
        exported_counts: Dict[str, int] = {
            self.canonical_labels.get(nid, nid): cnt
            for nid, cnt in self.pair_counter.items()
        }
        exported_files: Dict[str, List[str]] = {
            self.canonical_labels.get(nid, nid): sorted(paths)
            for nid, paths in self.files_by_name.items()
        }
        payload: Dict[str, Any] = {
            "fichiers_traites": sorted(self.already_processed),
            "idx_fichier": self.file_idx,
            "nb_lots_ecrits": self.written_batches,
            "cache_auto": self.auto_cache,
            "compteurs": self.counters,
            "compteur_paires": exported_counts,
            "fichiers_par_nom": exported_files,
        }
        DocumentTextUtils.atomic_write_text(
            self.config.resume_state_file,
            json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2),
        )

    def pseudonymize_id(self, raw_val: str, category: str) -> str:
        """Remplace un téléphone ou e-mail par son alias ou un jeton SHA-256."""
        norm_id: str = DocumentTextUtils.normalize_identifier(raw_val)
        if norm_id in self.rules.contacts_id:
            return self.rules.contacts_id[norm_id]
        salted: bytes = (norm_id + self.config.secret_salt).encode("utf-8")
        digest: str = hashlib.sha256(salted).hexdigest()[:10]
        if digest not in self.auto_cache:
            self.auto_cache[digest] = (
                f"{category}_{self.counters[category]:03d}"
            )
            self.counters[category] += 1
        return self.auto_cache[digest]
    def scan_residual_names(self, text: str, display_path: str) -> None:
        """Détecte et regroupe les noms propres résiduels sous forme canonique."""
        for m in self.PAIR_PATTERN.finditer(text):
            p1, p2 = m.group(1), m.group(2)
            if not self._is_plausible_pair(p1, p2):
                continue
            norm_id, _ = self._canonicalize_key(f"{p1} {p2}")
            self.pair_counter[norm_id] += 1
            self.files_by_name[norm_id].add(display_path)

        for m in self.TITLE_PATTERN.finditer(text):
            name: str = m.group(1)
            norm_name: str = DocumentTextUtils.normalize_word(name)
            if (
                self._is_valid_word(name)
                and len(norm_name) >= 3
                and norm_name not in self.rules.full_targets
            ):
                norm_id, _ = self._canonicalize_key(name)
                self.pair_counter[norm_id] += 1
                self.files_by_name[norm_id].add(display_path)

    @staticmethod
    def _audit_sort_key(entry: Tuple[str, str, int]) -> Tuple[int, str]:
        """Calcule la clé de tri (occurrences décroissantes, puis libellé)."""
        _, display_name, count = entry
        return (-count, display_name.lower())

    def _format_audit_entry(
        self, norm_id: str, display_name: str, count: int, pad_width: int
    ) -> Tuple[str, str]:
        """Formate la ligne complète d'audit et la ligne épurée pour copier-coller."""
        short_names: List[str] = sorted(
            {os.path.basename(p) for p in self.files_by_name[norm_id]}
        )
        preview: str = ", ".join(short_names[:3])
        extra: int = len(short_names) - 3
        if extra > 0:
            preview += f" (+{extra} autre(s))"

        suggestion = self._suggest_alias_from_fuzzy_last_name(display_name)
        if suggestion:
            suggested_alias, close_last = suggestion
            rule_col: str = f"{display_name:<{pad_width}} = {suggested_alias}"
            detailed: str = (
                f"{rule_col}   # [{count:>3}x] "
                f"(Proche de '{close_last}') {preview}\n"
            )
            copy_line: str = f"{display_name} = {suggested_alias}\n"
            return detailed, copy_line

        rule_col = f"{display_name:<{pad_width}} = A_MASQUER"
        detailed = f"{rule_col}   # [{count:>3}x] {preview}\n"
        copy_line = f"{display_name} = \n"
        return detailed, copy_line

    def write_report(self) -> int:
        """Actualise simultanément audit_noms_residuels.txt et audit_noms_residuels_copier.txt."""
        manual_name: str = os.path.basename(self.config.manual_contacts_file)
        copy_name: str = os.path.basename(self.config.audit_copy_file)
        results: List[Tuple[str, str, int]] = sorted(
            [
                (nid, self.canonical_labels.get(nid, nid), cnt)
                for nid, cnt in self.pair_counter.items()
                if cnt >= self.config.min_audit_occurrences
            ],
            key=self._audit_sort_key,
        )
        sep_main: str = "=" * 78
        sep_sub: str = "-" * 78
        report_lines: List[str] = [
            f"# {sep_main}\n",
            f"# RAPPORT D'AUDIT DES NOMS PROPRES RÉSIDUELS\n",
            f"# Fichiers analysés : {len(self.already_processed)} | "
            f"Noms détectés : {len(results)} | "
            f"Seuil min : {self.config.min_audit_occurrences}x\n",
            f"# {sep_main}\n",
            "# MODE D'EMPLOI :\n",
            f"#   1. Ouvrez '{copy_name}' pour récupérer directement les "
            "lignes 'Prénom Nom = ' sans texte superflu.\n",
            f"#   2. Complétez l'alias (ex: Prénom I.) et collez-les "
            f"dans '{manual_name}' (jamais écrasé).\n",
            "#   3. Ajoutez les faux positifs récurrents dans "
            "'excluded_words' (config.ini).\n",
            f"# {sep_sub}\n\n",
        ]
        copy_lines: List[str] = []

        if not results:
            report_lines.append("Aucun nouveau nom propre résiduel probable détecté.\n")
        else:
            pad_width: int = max(len(disp) for _, disp, _ in results) + 1
            tiers: List[Tuple[str, List[Tuple[str, str, int]]]] = [
                (" PRIORITÉ HAUTE (5 occurrences et plus)", [(nid, disp, c) for nid, disp, c in results if c >= 5]),
                (" PRIORITÉ MOYENNE (2 à 4 occurrences)", [(nid, disp, c) for nid, disp, c in results if 2 <= c <= 4]),
                (" OCCURRENCES UNIQUES (1 seule apparition)", [(nid, disp, c) for nid, disp, c in results if c == 1]),
            ]
            for title, items in tiers:
                if not items:
                    continue
                report_lines.append(f"# {sep_sub}\n# {title} — {len(items)} entrée(s)\n# {sep_sub}\n")
                for nid, disp, count in items:
                    det_ln, cp_ln = self._format_audit_entry(nid, disp, count, pad_width)
                    report_lines.append(det_ln)
                    copy_lines.append(cp_ln)
                report_lines.append("\n")

        DocumentTextUtils.atomic_write_text(self.config.audit_report_file, "".join(report_lines))
        DocumentTextUtils.atomic_write_text(self.config.audit_copy_file, "".join(copy_lines))
        return len(results)

FICHIER : doc_anonymizer/comm_extractors.py
================================================================================
# Commandes pip à exécuter : pip install faster-whisper numpy scipy

from collections import defaultdict
import datetime
import email
from email.header import decode_header
from email.message import Message
from email.utils import getaddresses, parsedate_to_datetime
import mailbox
import os
import re
import sqlite3
from typing import Any, DefaultDict, Dict, Iterable, List, Optional, Set, Tuple
import xml.etree.ElementTree as ET
from faster_whisper import WhisperModel
import numpy as np
from scipy.io import wavfile
from doc_anonymizer.config_manager import PipelineConfig
from doc_anonymizer.office_extractors import OfficeAndDocumentExtractor
from doc_anonymizer.text_utils import DocumentTextUtils


class CommunicationExtractor:
    """
    Gère l'extraction des messages SMS/MMS (XML), des e-mails (.mbox/.eml),
    la transcription vocale (Whisper) et l'extraction Audacity (.aup3).
    """

    MAIN_CHARACTER_TOKEN: str = "{MAIN_CHARACTER}"

    def __init__(
        self,
        config: PipelineConfig,
        doc_extractor: OfficeAndDocumentExtractor,
    ) -> None:
        """
        Initialise l'extracteur sans charger immédiatement Whisper en RAM.

        :param config: Instance de PipelineConfig.
        :param doc_extractor: Extracteur PDF pour les pièces jointes.
        """
        self.config: PipelineConfig = config
        self.doc_extractor: OfficeAndDocumentExtractor = doc_extractor
        self._whisper_model: Optional[WhisperModel] = None
        self._whisper_cache_dir: str = os.path.join(
            os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)),
            "..",
            ".whisper_cache",
        )

    @property
    def whisper_model(self) -> Optional[WhisperModel]:
        """
        Charge le modèle Whisper uniquement au premier fichier audio rencontré.

        :return: Instance de WhisperModel ou None si l'audio est désactivé.
        """
        if not self.config.enable_audio:
            return None
        if self._whisper_model is None:
            cache_dir: str = os.path.normpath(self._whisper_cache_dir)
            os.makedirs(cache_dir, exist_ok=True)
            print(f"Chargement du modèle audio Whisper '{self.config.whisper_model}'...", flush=True)
            try:
                self._whisper_model = WhisperModel(
                    self.config.whisper_model, device="cpu", compute_type="int8",
                    download_root=cache_dir, local_files_only=True,
                )
            except Exception:
                self._whisper_model = WhisperModel(
                    self.config.whisper_model, device="cpu", compute_type="int8",
                    download_root=cache_dir, local_files_only=False,
                )
        return self._whisper_model
    @staticmethod
    def decode_mime_header(raw_val: Optional[str]) -> str:
        """
        Décode un en-tête MIME en chaîne Unicode.

        :param raw_val: Valeur brute de l'en-tête.
        :return: Texte décodé.
        """
        if not raw_val:
            return ""
        parts: List[str] = []
        for chunk, enc in decode_header(raw_val):
            if isinstance(chunk, bytes):
                parts.append(chunk.decode(enc or "utf-8", errors="ignore"))
            else:
                parts.append(str(chunk))
        return "".join(parts).strip()

    @staticmethod
    def _extract_email_body(msg: Message) -> str:
        """
        Extrait le corps texte principal (text/plain ou repli text/html) d'un message MIME.

        :param msg: Objet email.message.Message.
        :return: Texte nettoyé du corps du message.
        """
        plain_parts: List[str] = []
        html_parts: List[str] = []
        if msg.is_multipart():
            for part in msg.walk():
                ctype: str = part.get_content_type()
                disp: str = str(part.get("Content-Disposition") or "")
                if "attachment" in disp.lower():
                    continue
                if ctype in ("text/plain", "text/html"):
                    payload = part.get_payload(decode=True)
                    if not isinstance(payload, bytes):
                        continue
                    charset: str = part.get_content_charset() or "utf-8"
                    decoded: str = payload.decode(charset, errors="ignore")
                    (plain_parts if ctype == "text/plain" else html_parts).append(decoded)
        else:
            payload = msg.get_payload(decode=True)
            if isinstance(payload, bytes):
                charset = msg.get_content_charset() or "utf-8"
                decoded = payload.decode(charset, errors="ignore")
                (html_parts if msg.get_content_type() == "text/html" else plain_parts).append(decoded)

        if plain_parts:
            return "\n".join(plain_parts).strip()
        if html_parts:
            return DocumentTextUtils.html_to_plain_text("\n".join(html_parts)).strip()
        return ""

    def _extract_email_pdf_attachments(
        self, msg: Message, source_label: str
    ) -> List[str]:
        """
        Extrait le texte des pièces jointes PDF d'un message MIME en assainissant leur nom.

        :param msg: Objet email.message.Message.
        :param source_label: Nom du fichier source pour l'affichage.
        :return: Liste des textes extraits des pièces jointes PDF.
        """
        attachments_txt: List[str] = []
        if not msg.is_multipart():
            return attachments_txt

        for part in msg.walk():
            raw_file_name: Optional[str] = part.get_filename()
            file_name: str = self.decode_mime_header(raw_file_name)
            ctype: str = part.get_content_type()
            if ctype == "application/pdf" or file_name.lower().endswith(".pdf"):
                pdf_bytes = part.get_payload(decode=True)
                if isinstance(pdf_bytes, bytes) and pdf_bytes:
                    safe_name: str = re.sub(r"[\r\n<>:\"/\\|?*]", "_", file_name).strip()
                    disp_name: str = safe_name or f"piece_jointe_{source_label}.pdf"
                    pdf_txt, err = self.doc_extractor.extract_pdf(
                        pdf_source=pdf_bytes, display_name=disp_name, is_stream=True
                    )
                    if pdf_txt and not err:
                        attachments_txt.append(pdf_txt)
        return attachments_txt
    def extract_emails(
        self, file_path: str, allowed_emails: Set[str], discovered_emails: Set[str]
    ) -> Tuple[Dict[str, List[str]], Optional[str]]:
        """
        Extrait en streaming les e-mails d'un fichier .mbox ou .eml et collecte
        les couples Nom d'affichage + e-mail pour enrichissement automatique.

        :param file_path: Chemin vers le fichier .mbox ou .eml.
        :param allowed_emails: Ensemble des e-mails filtrés depuis contacts.txt.
        :param discovered_emails: Collecteur global des e-mails et noms rencontrés.
        :return: Tuple (dictionnaire_par_mois, message_erreur).
        """
        file_name: str = os.path.basename(file_path)
        is_eml: bool = file_name.lower().endswith(".eml")
        eml_filename_period: Optional[str] = DocumentTextUtils.detect_period_in_text(file_name) if is_eml else None
        by_month: DefaultDict[str, List[str]] = defaultdict(list)
        mbox_handle: Optional[mailbox.mbox] = None

        try:
            if is_eml:
                with open(file_path, "rb") as f_eml:
                    msg_iter: Iterable[Message] = [email.message_from_binary_file(f_eml)]
            else:
                mbox_handle = mailbox.mbox(file_path)
                msg_iter = mbox_handle

            scanned_count: int = 0
            matched_count: int = 0
            for msg in msg_iter:
                scanned_count += 1
                if not is_eml and scanned_count % 500 == 0:
                    print(f"    ... {file_name} : {scanned_count} e-mail(s) ({matched_count} retenu(s)) ...", flush=True)

                raw_headers: List[str] = [self.decode_mime_header(msg.get(h)) for h in ("From", "To", "Cc", "Bcc", "Reply-To")]
                msg_emails: Set[str] = set()
                for disp_raw, addr in getaddresses(raw_headers):
                    if not addr or "@" not in addr:
                        continue
                    norm_addr: str = DocumentTextUtils.normalize_identifier(addr)
                    msg_emails.add(norm_addr)
                    clean_disp: str = re.sub(r"[\r\n=|]+", " ", disp_raw).strip().strip('"').strip()
                    if clean_disp and "@" not in clean_disp and len(clean_disp.split()) >= 2:
                        discovered_emails.add(f"{norm_addr}|{clean_disp}")
                    else:
                        discovered_emails.add(norm_addr)

                if allowed_emails and not (msg_emails & allowed_emails):
                    continue

                matched_count += 1
                raw_date: str = str(msg.get("Date") or "").strip()
                period: str = eml_filename_period or "0000-00"
                date_str: str = raw_date or "Date inconnue"
                if raw_date:
                    try:
                        dt = parsedate_to_datetime(raw_date)
                        date_str = dt.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
                        if not eml_filename_period:
                            period = dt.strftime("%Y-%m")
                    except Exception:
                        if not eml_filename_period:
                            detected = DocumentTextUtils.detect_period_in_text(raw_date)
                            if detected:
                                period = detected

                block_lines: List[str] = [
                    "========================================\n",
                    f"EMAIL ({file_name}) | DATE : {date_str}\n",
                    f"DE : {self.decode_mime_header(msg.get('From'))}\n",
                    f"À : {self.decode_mime_header(msg.get('To'))}\n",
                    f"OBJET : {self.decode_mime_header(msg.get('Subject'))}\n",
                    "----------------------------------------\n",
                    f"{self._extract_email_body(msg)}\n\n",
                ]
                pdf_att: List[str] = self._extract_email_pdf_attachments(msg, file_name)
                if pdf_att:
                    block_lines.extend(pdf_att)
                by_month[period].append("".join(block_lines))

            if not is_eml:
                print(f"    -> Fin {file_name} : {matched_count}/{scanned_count} e-mail(s) retenu(s).", flush=True)
            return dict(by_month), None
        except Exception as exc:
            return {}, f"Erreur lecture e-mail ({exc})"
        finally:
            if mbox_handle is not None:
                mbox_handle.close()
    @staticmethod
    def _validate_safe_xml_header(xml_path: str) -> None:
        """
        Bloque toute déclaration DOCTYPE ou ENTITY (protection XXE / Billion Laughs).

        :param xml_path: Chemin vers le fichier XML.
        """
        with open(xml_path, "rb") as f_head:
            head_upper: bytes = f_head.read(4096).upper()
        if b"<!DOCTYPE" in head_upper or b"<!ENTITY" in head_upper:
            raise ValueError("Déclaration DOCTYPE/ENTITY interdite par sécurité dans le XML.")

    def parse_sms_xml(
        self, xml_path: str, target_phones: Dict[str, str], already_processed: Set[str],
        discovered_phones: Set[str], discovered_emails: Optional[Set[str]] = None,
    ) -> Tuple[Dict[str, List[Tuple[int, str]]], List[str]]:
        """
        Parcourt en streaming un export XML SMS/MMS.

        :param xml_path: Chemin vers le fichier XML.
        :param target_phones: Dictionnaire numéro normalisé -> alias.
        :param already_processed: Clés 'fichier|numero' déjà extraites.
        :param discovered_phones: Collecteur global des numéros rencontrés.
        :param discovered_emails: Collecteur optionnel des e-mails rencontrés.
        :return: Tuple (messages_par_mois, liste_erreurs).
        """
        file_name: str = os.path.basename(xml_path)
        by_month: DefaultDict[str, List[Tuple[int, str]]] = defaultdict(list)
        errors: List[str] = []
        try:
            self._validate_safe_xml_header(xml_path)
            for _, elem in ET.iterparse(xml_path, events=("end",)):
                if elem.tag not in ("sms", "mms"):
                    continue
                matched_aliases: List[str] = []
                for norm_addr in DocumentTextUtils.split_sms_addresses(elem.attrib.get("address", "")):
                    if "@" in norm_addr:
                        if discovered_emails is not None and not norm_addr.lower().endswith("://google.com"):
                            discovered_emails.add(norm_addr)
                    else:
                        discovered_phones.add(norm_addr)
                    if norm_addr in target_phones and f"{file_name}|{norm_addr}" not in already_processed:
                        if target_phones[norm_addr] not in matched_aliases:
                            matched_aliases.append(target_phones[norm_addr])

                if not matched_aliases:
                    elem.clear()
                    continue

                alias: str = ", ".join(matched_aliases)
                raw_ts: str = elem.attrib.get("date", "0")
                ts_ms: int = int(raw_ts) if raw_ts.isdigit() else 0
                dt = datetime.datetime.fromtimestamp(ts_ms / 1000.0)
                direction: str = (
                    f"[ENVOYÉ par {self.MAIN_CHARACTER_TOKEN} à {alias}]"
                    if elem.attrib.get("type", elem.attrib.get("msg_box", "1")) == "2"
                    else f"[REÇU par {self.MAIN_CHARACTER_TOKEN} de {alias}]"
                )
                body: str = elem.attrib.get("body", "").strip() if elem.tag == "sms" else "\n".join(
                    p.attrib.get("text", "").strip() for p in elem.iter("part")
                    if p.attrib.get("ct") == "text/plain" and p.attrib.get("text", "").strip()
                ).strip()
                if body:
                    by_month[dt.strftime("%Y-%m")].append(
                        (ts_ms, f"[{dt.strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] {direction}\n{body}\n----------------------------------------\n")
                    )
                elem.clear()
            return dict(by_month), errors
        except Exception as exc:
            errors.append(f"{file_name} -> Erreur parsing XML ({exc})")
            return {}, errors

    def extract_audio(self, file_path: str) -> Tuple[Optional[str], Optional[str]]:
        """
        Transcrit un fichier audio via faster-whisper.

        :param file_path: Chemin vers le fichier audio.
        :return: Tuple (transcription_formatee, message_erreur).
        """
        model: Optional[WhisperModel] = self.whisper_model
        if not self.config.enable_audio or not model:
            return None, "Transcription audio désactivée"
        file_name: str = os.path.basename(file_path)
        phone_match = DocumentTextUtils.REGEX_PHONE.search(file_name)
        part_line: str = f"PARTICIPANTS : {self.MAIN_CHARACTER_TOKEN} et {phone_match.group(0)}\n" if phone_match else ""
        try:
            segments, _ = model.transcribe(file_path, beam_size=5, vad_filter=True)
            lines: List[str] = []
            for seg in segments:
                txt: str = seg.text.strip()
                if txt:
                    mins, secs = divmod(int(seg.start), 60)
                    lines.append(f"[{mins:02d}:{secs:02d}] {txt}")
            if not lines:
                return None, "Aucune parole détectée dans l'audio"
            return f"========================================\nAUDIO : {file_name}\n{part_line}----------------------------------------\n" + "\n".join(lines) + "\n\n", None
        except Exception as exc:
            return None, f"Erreur transcription audio ({exc})"

    def extract_audacity_aup3(self, file_path: str) -> Tuple[Optional[str], Optional[str]]:
        """
        Extrait et transcrit la piste audio principale d'un projet Audacity (.aup3).

        :param file_path: Chemin vers le fichier .aup3.
        :return: Tuple (transcription_formatee, message_erreur).
        """
        if not self.config.enable_audio or not self.whisper_model:
            return None, "Transcription audio désactivée"
        file_name: str = os.path.basename(file_path)
        temp_wav: str = f"{file_path}.tmp_extract.wav"
        conn: Optional[sqlite3.Connection] = None
        try:
            conn = sqlite3.connect(file_path)
            cur = conn.cursor()
            cur.execute("SELECT samples FROM sampleblocks ORDER BY blockid")
            rows: List[Any] = cur.fetchall()
            if not rows:
                return None, "Projet Audacity sans bloc audio"
            audio_arr = np.frombuffer(b"".join(next(iter(r)) for r in rows if r and next(iter(r))), dtype=np.float32)
            if audio_arr.size == 0:
                return None, "Piste audio Audacity vide"
            wavfile.write(temp_wav, 44100, audio_arr)
            res_txt, err = self.extract_audio(temp_wav)
            return (res_txt.replace(os.path.basename(temp_wav), file_name) if res_txt else None), err
        except Exception as exc:
            return None, f"Erreur lecture projet Audacity ({exc})"
        finally:
            if conn is not None:
                try:
                    conn.close()
                except sqlite3.Error:
                    pass
            if os.path.exists(temp_wav):
                try:
                    os.remove(temp_wav)
                except OSError:
                    pass

================================================================================
FICHIER : doc_anonymizer/config_manager.py
================================================================================
# Commandes pip à exécuter : Aucune dépendance externe requise

import configparser
import os
import secrets
from typing import FrozenSet, Set
from common.core_utils import SharedCoreUtils
from google_contacts_parser.geo_data import FrenchGeoCatalog


class PipelineConfig:
    """
    Charge et expose de manière typée les paramètres techniques d'extraction
    et d'anonymisation depuis le fichier config.ini unifié, en partageant
    prenoms.txt, villes_masquees.txt, mots_exclus.txt et les index de contacts.
    """

    DEFAULT_PLACEHOLDER_SALT: str = "cle_secrete_a_modifier_987"

    COMMON_WORD_LOCATIONS: FrozenSet[str] = frozenset({
        "reunion", "la reunion", "centre", "nord", "sud", "est", "ouest",
        "cher", "orne", "somme", "lot", "tarn", "var", "eure", "ain", "aude",
        "manche", "marne", "meuse", "nievre", "oise", "rhone", "sarthe",
        "savoie", "seine", "vendee", "vienne", "vosges", "yonne",
        "orange", "nice", "pau", "sens", "ales", "gap", "dax", "foix",
        "auch", "lozere", "creuse", "cantal", "landes", "jura", "doubs",
    })

    def __init__(self, config_path: str = "config.ini") -> None:
        """Initialise les valeurs par défaut puis charge le fichier INI."""
        self.config_path: str = os.path.abspath(config_path)
        self._base_dir: str = os.path.dirname(self.config_path)
        self._parser: configparser.ConfigParser = configparser.ConfigParser()

        self.root_dir: str = self._base_dir
        self.first_names_file: str = os.path.join(self._base_dir, "prenoms.txt")
        self.locations_file: str = os.path.join(self._base_dir, "villes_masquees.txt")
        self.excluded_words_file: str = os.path.join(self._base_dir, "mots_exclus.txt")
        self.contacts_file: str = os.path.join(self._base_dir, "contacts.txt")
        self.manual_contacts_file: str = os.path.join(self._base_dir, "contacts_manuels.txt")
        self.discovered_contacts_file: str = os.path.join(self._base_dir, "contacts_emails_decouverts.txt")
        self.indexed_contacts_file: str = os.path.join(self._base_dir, "contacts_indexes.txt")
        self.anonymized_contacts_file: str = os.path.join(self._base_dir, "contacts_anonymises.txt")
        self.all_sms_numbers_file: str = os.path.join(self._base_dir, "tous_numeros_sms.txt")
        self.all_emails_file: str = os.path.join(self._base_dir, "tous_emails_trouves.txt")
        self.sources_dir: str = os.path.join(self._base_dir, "fichiers sources")
        self.transcriptions_dir: str = os.path.join(self._base_dir, "transcriptions")
        self.results_dir: str = os.path.join(self._base_dir, "resultats_anonymisation")
        self.audit_report_file: str = os.path.join(self._base_dir, "audit_noms_residuels.txt")
