        self.audit_copy_file: str = os.path.join(self._base_dir, "audit_noms_residuels_copier.txt")
        self.enable_ocr: bool = True
        self.enable_audio: bool = True
        self.whisper_model: str = "base"
        self.defer_heavy_files: bool = True
        self.heavy_file_threshold_mb: float = 25.0
        self.extraction_workers: int = 0
        self.ignored_dirs: Set[str] = {
            "transcriptions", "resultats_anonymisation",
            "venv", ".venv", ".git", "__pycache__", "common", "tools", "tests",
        }
        self.main_character_name: str = "Personnage_Principal"
        self.file_prefix: str = "anonymise_"
        self.secret_salt: str = self.DEFAULT_PLACEHOLDER_SALT
        self.max_batch_bytes: int = 180 * 1024
        self.max_batch_files: int = 50
        self.files_per_folder: int = 10
        self.min_audit_occurrences: int = 1
        self.mask_ssn: bool = True
        self.mask_addresses: bool = True
        self.mask_urls: bool = True
        self.mask_financial_and_admin_ids: bool = True
        self.masked_locations: Set[str] = set()
        self.known_first_names: Set[str] = set()
        self.excluded_words: Set[str] = set()

        self._load_french_geo_catalog()
        self._load()
        self.load_external_lists()
        self._ensure_strong_secret_salt()

        self.sms_tracking_file: str = os.path.join(self.transcriptions_dir, ".sms_numeros_traites.txt")
        self.extraction_tracking_file: str = os.path.join(self.transcriptions_dir, ".fichiers_extraits.txt")
        self.resume_state_file: str = os.path.join(self.results_dir, ".etat_anonymisation.json")

    def load_external_lists(self) -> None:
        """Charge ou recharge les listes externes (villes, mots exclus, prénoms)."""
        self.masked_locations.update(
            SharedCoreUtils.load_locations_set(self.locations_file)
        )
        self.excluded_words.update(
            SharedCoreUtils.load_words_set(self.excluded_words_file)
        )
        self._expand_location_variants()
        self.known_first_names.update(
            SharedCoreUtils.load_first_names_set(self.first_names_file)
        )

    def _load_french_geo_catalog(self) -> None:
        """Charge les départements, régions et grandes villes depuis FrenchGeoCatalog."""
        for attr_name in dir(FrenchGeoCatalog):
            if attr_name.startswith("_"):
                continue
            val = getattr(FrenchGeoCatalog, attr_name)
            if isinstance(val, (set, list, tuple)):
                for item in val:
                    if isinstance(item, str) and len(item.strip()) >= 3:
                        norm_loc: str = SharedCoreUtils.normalize_token(item)
                        if norm_loc not in self.COMMON_WORD_LOCATIONS:
                            self.masked_locations.add(norm_loc)

    def _expand_location_variants(self) -> None:
        """Ajoute les variantes avec espaces à la place des tirets pour les villes composées."""
        expanded: Set[str] = set()
        for loc in self.masked_locations:
            if "-" in loc:
                expanded.add(loc.replace("-", " "))
        self.masked_locations.update(expanded)

    def _ensure_strong_secret_salt(self) -> None:
        """Génère et persiste un sel cryptographique aléatoire de 256 bits dans .secret_salt."""
        if self.secret_salt and self.secret_salt != self.DEFAULT_PLACEHOLDER_SALT:
            return
        salt_file: str = os.path.join(self._base_dir, ".secret_salt")
        if os.path.exists(salt_file):
            try:
                with open(salt_file, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f_s:
                    stored: str = f_s.read().strip()
                if len(stored) >= 32:
                    self.secret_salt = stored
                    return
            except OSError:
                pass
        generated: str = secrets.token_hex(32)
        self.secret_salt = generated
        try:
            SharedCoreUtils.atomic_write_text(salt_file, f"{generated}\n")
        except OSError:
            pass
    def _resolve_path(self, raw_path: str) -> str:
        """Résout un chemin relativement au dossier contenant config.ini."""
        cleaned: str = raw_path.strip()
        if os.path.isabs(cleaned):
            return os.path.normpath(cleaned)
        return os.path.normpath(os.path.join(self._base_dir, cleaned))

    @staticmethod
    def _parse_csv_set(raw_value: str) -> Set[str]:
        """Convertit une chaîne séparée par des virgules en ensemble nettoyé."""
        return {item.strip() for item in raw_value.split(",") if item.strip()}

    def _load(self) -> None:
        """Lit les sections partagées et spécifiques de config.ini."""
        if not os.path.exists(self.config_path):
            return

        self._parser.read(self.config_path, encoding="utf-8")
        if "files" in self._parser:
            files_sec = self._parser["files"]
            path_keys = {
                "first_names_file": "first_names_file",
                "locations_file": "locations_file",
                "excluded_words_file": "excluded_words_file",
                "output_file": "indexed_contacts_file",
                "anonymized_output_file": "anonymized_contacts_file",
                "inverted_output_file": "contacts_file",
                "manual_contacts_file": "manual_contacts_file",
                "discovered_contacts_file": "discovered_contacts_file",
            }
            for ini_key, attr_name in path_keys.items():
                raw_val: str = files_sec.get(ini_key, fallback="").strip()
                if raw_val:
                    setattr(self, attr_name, self._resolve_path(raw_val))

        if "anonymizer" in self._parser:
            anon_sec = self._parser["anonymizer"]
            if "extra_locations" in anon_sec:
                self.masked_locations.update(
                    SharedCoreUtils.normalize_token(loc)
                    for loc in self._parse_csv_set(anon_sec["extra_locations"])
                )

        if "paths" in self._parser:
            paths_sec = self._parser["paths"]
            for key in (
                "root_dir", "locations_file", "excluded_words_file",
                "contacts_file", "manual_contacts_file",
                "discovered_contacts_file", "all_sms_numbers_file",
                "all_emails_file", "sources_dir", "transcriptions_dir",
                "results_dir", "audit_report_file", "audit_copy_file",
            ):
                if key in paths_sec:
                    setattr(self, key, self._resolve_path(paths_sec[key]))

        if "extraction" in self._parser:
            ext_sec = self._parser["extraction"]
            self.enable_ocr = ext_sec.getboolean("enable_ocr", fallback=self.enable_ocr)
            self.enable_audio = ext_sec.getboolean("enable_audio", fallback=self.enable_audio)
            self.whisper_model = ext_sec.get("whisper_model", fallback=self.whisper_model)
            self.defer_heavy_files = ext_sec.getboolean("defer_heavy_files", fallback=self.defer_heavy_files)
            self.heavy_file_threshold_mb = ext_sec.getfloat(
                "heavy_file_threshold_mb", fallback=self.heavy_file_threshold_mb
            )
            self.extraction_workers = ext_sec.getint("extraction_workers", fallback=self.extraction_workers)
            if "ignored_dirs" in ext_sec:
                self.ignored_dirs = self._parse_csv_set(ext_sec["ignored_dirs"])

        if "anonymization" in self._parser:
            anon_doc_sec = self._parser["anonymization"]
            raw_main: str = anon_doc_sec.get("main_character_name", fallback=self.main_character_name).strip()
            if raw_main:
                self.main_character_name = raw_main
            self.file_prefix = anon_doc_sec.get("file_prefix", fallback=self.file_prefix)
            self.secret_salt = anon_doc_sec.get("secret_salt", fallback=self.secret_salt)
            self.max_batch_bytes = anon_doc_sec.getint("max_batch_bytes", fallback=self.max_batch_bytes)
            self.max_batch_files = anon_doc_sec.getint("max_batch_files", fallback=self.max_batch_files)
            self.files_per_folder = anon_doc_sec.getint("files_per_folder", fallback=self.files_per_folder)
            self.min_audit_occurrences = anon_doc_sec.getint(
                "min_audit_occurrences", fallback=self.min_audit_occurrences
            )
            self.mask_ssn = anon_doc_sec.getboolean("mask_ssn", fallback=self.mask_ssn)
            self.mask_addresses = anon_doc_sec.getboolean("mask_addresses", fallback=self.mask_addresses)
            self.mask_urls = anon_doc_sec.getboolean("mask_urls", fallback=self.mask_urls)
            self.mask_financial_and_admin_ids = anon_doc_sec.getboolean(
                "mask_financial_and_admin_ids", fallback=self.mask_financial_and_admin_ids
            )
            if "masked_locations" in anon_doc_sec:
                self.masked_locations.update(
                    SharedCoreUtils.normalize_token(loc)
                    for loc in self._parse_csv_set(anon_doc_sec["masked_locations"])
                )
            if "known_first_names" in anon_doc_sec:
                self.known_first_names.update(
                    SharedCoreUtils.normalize_token(w)
                    for w in self._parse_csv_set(anon_doc_sec["known_first_names"])
                )
            if "excluded_words" in anon_doc_sec:
                self.excluded_words.update(
                    SharedCoreUtils.normalize_token(w)
                    for w in self._parse_csv_set(anon_doc_sec["excluded_words"])
                )

================================================================================
FICHIER : doc_anonymizer/contact_rules_loader.py
================================================================================
# Commandes pip à exécuter : Aucune dépendance externe requise

from collections import defaultdict
import os
import re
from typing import DefaultDict, Dict, List, Optional, Pattern, Set
from doc_anonymizer.config_manager import PipelineConfig
from doc_anonymizer.text_utils import DocumentTextUtils


class ContactRulesLoader:
    """
    Charge les règles d'anonymisation depuis tous les fichiers de contacts,
    génère les variantes OCR (y compris collées sans espaces, St/Saint
    et confusions chiffres/lettres) et compile les regex optimisées (Trie).
    """

    OCR_DIGIT_TRANS = str.maketrans({
        "0": "o", "1": "i", "5": "s", "8": "b", "4": "a", "6": "g",
    })

    COMMON_WORD_COLLISIONS = frozenset({
        "gard", "ard", "indre", "aube", "gers", "var", "ain", "eure",
        "orne", "lot", "cher", "tarn", "nord", "sud", "est", "ouest",
        "laize", "fay", "hom", "port", "pont", "mont", "val", "bois",
    })

    def __init__(
        self, config: PipelineConfig, manual_only: bool = False
    ) -> None:
        """Initialise les structures d'indexation des règles de contacts."""
        self.config: PipelineConfig = config
        self.manual_only: bool = manual_only
        self.contacts_id: Dict[str, str] = {}
        self.keyword_to_alias: Dict[str, str] = {}
        self.glued_to_alias: Dict[str, str] = {}
        self.alias_words: Set[str] = set()
        self.full_targets: Set[str] = set()
        self.protected_surnames: Set[str] = set()
        self.surname_to_alias: Dict[str, str] = {}
        self.last_name_to_aliases: DefaultDict[str, Set[str]] = defaultdict(set)
        self.last_name_display: Dict[str, str] = {}
        self.combined_keyword_pattern: Optional[Pattern[str]] = None
        self.combined_glued_pattern: Optional[Pattern[str]] = None
        self.combined_location_pattern: Optional[Pattern[str]] = None
        self._normalized_first_names: Set[str] = {
            DocumentTextUtils.normalize_word(fn)
            for fn in config.known_first_names
        }

    @classmethod
    def normalize_ocr_key(cls, text: str) -> str:
        """Normalise une chaîne en unifiant les confusions chiffres/lettres OCR."""
        base: str = DocumentTextUtils.normalize_word(text).translate(
            cls.OCR_DIGIT_TRANS
        )
        return re.sub(r"[\s\-'\u2019\._]+", "", base)

    def is_first_name(self, token: str) -> bool:
        """Vérifie si un jeton est un prénom connu (simple ou composé)."""
        norm: str = DocumentTextUtils.normalize_word(token)
        if not self._normalized_first_names and self.config.known_first_names:
            self._normalized_first_names = {
                DocumentTextUtils.normalize_word(fn)
                for fn in self.config.known_first_names
            }
        if norm in self._normalized_first_names:
            return True
        if "-" in norm:
            parts: List[str] = [p for p in norm.split("-") if p]
            return bool(parts) and all(
                p in self._normalized_first_names for p in parts
            )
        return False
    def _register_rule(
        self,
        raw_keyword: str,
        raw_alias: str,
        norm_id: str = "",
        is_manual: bool = False,
    ) -> None:
        """Enregistre une règle (cible -> alias) et ses variantes OCR/noms de famille."""
        kw: str = raw_keyword.strip()
        al: str = raw_alias.strip()
        if len(kw) < 2 or not al:
            return

        for w in re.findall(r"[A-Za-zÉÈÊËÀÂÄÎÏÔÖÙÛÜÇéèêëàâäîïôöùûüç'-]+", al):
            nw: str = DocumentTextUtils.normalize_word(w)
            if len(nw) >= 2:
                self.alias_words.add(nw)

        if norm_id:
            if norm_id not in self.contacts_id or is_manual:
                self.contacts_id[norm_id] = al
            return

        if not is_manual:
            kw_words: List[str] = [
                w for w in re.split(r"[\s\-'\u2019]+", kw) if w
            ]
            first_kw_word: str = next(iter(kw_words), "")
            if len(kw_words) == 1 and self.is_first_name(first_kw_word):
                return

        norm_kw: str = kw.lower()
        if norm_kw not in self.keyword_to_alias or is_manual:
            self.keyword_to_alias[norm_kw] = al
        self.full_targets.add(DocumentTextUtils.normalize_word(kw))

        words: List[str] = [w for w in kw.split() if len(w) >= 2]
        if len(words) >= 2:
            glued_key: str = self.normalize_ocr_key(kw)
            if len(glued_key) >= 7:
                self.glued_to_alias[glued_key] = al
                rev_key: str = self.normalize_ocr_key(
                    " ".join(reversed(words))
                )
                if len(rev_key) >= 7:
                    self.glued_to_alias[rev_key] = al

        self._extract_surname_protection(kw, al, is_manual=is_manual)

    def _extract_surname_protection(
        self, kw: str, al: str, is_manual: bool = False
    ) -> None:
        """Protège les noms de famille contenus dans une règle connue."""
        words: List[str] = [
            w for w in re.split(r"[\s\-'\u2019]+", kw) if len(w) >= 2
        ]
        if not words:
            return
        for idx, word in enumerate(words):
            norm_w: str = DocumentTextUtils.normalize_word(word)
            is_last_pos: bool = len(words) >= 2 and idx == len(words) - 1
            if (
                len(norm_w) >= 4
                and (not self.is_first_name(word) or is_last_pos)
                and norm_w not in self.config.excluded_words
                and norm_w not in self.config.masked_locations
                and norm_w not in DocumentTextUtils.ORG_GENERIC_WORDS
            ):
                self.protected_surnames.add(norm_w)
                self.last_name_to_aliases[norm_w].add(al)
                if norm_w not in self.last_name_display:
                    self.last_name_display[norm_w] = word.capitalize()
                short_alias: str = (
                    al if is_manual and len(words) == 1 else f"{word[:1].upper()}."
                )
                if norm_w not in self.surname_to_alias:
                    self.surname_to_alias[norm_w] = short_alias

    def _load_indexed_and_anonymized_pairs(self) -> None:
        """Relie les noms complets de contacts_indexes.txt aux alias de contacts_anonymises.txt."""
        idx_p: str = self.config.indexed_contacts_file
        ano_p: str = self.config.anonymized_contacts_file
        if not (os.path.exists(idx_p) and os.path.exists(ano_p)):
            return
        id_names: DefaultDict[str, List[str]] = defaultdict(list)
        with open(idx_p, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f_idx:
            for ln in f_idx:
                if "=" in ln and not ln.strip().startswith("#"):
                    l_p, r_p = ln.split("=", 1)
                    t, fn, nid = DocumentTextUtils.resolve_contact_pair(l_p, r_p)
                    if t and fn and nid:
                        id_names[nid].append(fn)
        id_aliases: DefaultDict[str, List[str]] = defaultdict(list)
        with open(ano_p, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f_ano:
            for ln in f_ano:
                if "=" in ln and not ln.strip().startswith("#"):
                    l_p, r_p = ln.split("=", 1)
                    t, al, nid = DocumentTextUtils.resolve_contact_pair(l_p, r_p)
                    if t and al and nid:
                        id_aliases[nid].append(al)
        for nid, fns in id_names.items():
            als: List[str] = id_aliases.get(nid, [])
            if len(fns) == len(als):
                for fn, al in zip(fns, als):
                    self._register_rule(fn, al, is_manual=False)
                    parts = [p for p in fn.split() if len(p) >= 4]
                    if parts:
                        self.last_name_to_aliases[DocumentTextUtils.normalize_word(parts[-1])].add(al)
            elif len(als) == 1:
                single_al: str = next(iter(als))
                for fn in fns:
                    self._register_rule(fn, single_al, is_manual=False)
                    parts = [p for p in fn.split() if len(p) >= 4]
                    if parts:
                        self.last_name_to_aliases[DocumentTextUtils.normalize_word(parts[-1])].add(single_al)
    def _load_file_rules(self, file_path: str, is_manual: bool = False) -> int:
        """Charge un fichier de règles cible = alias."""
        if not os.path.exists(file_path):
            return 0
        loaded: int = 0
        with open(file_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            for raw_line in f:
                line: str = raw_line.strip()
                if not line or line.startswith("#"):
                    continue
                for sep in ("=", ";", ","):
                    if sep in line:
                        left_p, right_p = line.split(sep, 1)
                        target, alias, norm_id = DocumentTextUtils.resolve_contact_pair(left_p, right_p)
                        if target and alias:
                            self._register_rule(target, alias, norm_id=norm_id, is_manual=is_manual)
                            loaded += 1
                        break
        return loaded

    def _register_protected_surnames(self) -> None:
        """Enregistre les noms de famille protégés pour les remplacer par leur initiale."""
        for surname, short_alias in self.surname_to_alias.items():
            if surname not in self.keyword_to_alias:
                self.keyword_to_alias[surname] = short_alias
                self.keyword_to_alias[surname.capitalize()] = short_alias
                self.keyword_to_alias[surname.upper()] = short_alias

    def _expand_saint_locations(self) -> Set[str]:
        """Ajoute les variantes St/Ste pour les communes composées."""
        expanded: Set[str] = set()
        for loc in self.config.masked_locations:
            norm_loc: str = DocumentTextUtils.normalize_word(loc)
            if norm_loc in self.COMMON_WORD_COLLISIONS:
                continue
            expanded.add(loc)
            if "sainte" in loc:
                expanded.add(re.sub(r"\bsainte\b", "ste", loc))
            if "saint" in loc:
                expanded.add(re.sub(r"\bsaint\b", "st", loc))
        return expanded

    def _compile_patterns(self) -> None:
        """Compile les expressions régulières Trie pour les mots-clés, formes collées et lieux."""
        if self.keyword_to_alias:
            kw_regex: str = DocumentTextUtils.build_ocr_tolerant_trie_regex(list(self.keyword_to_alias.keys()))
            self.combined_keyword_pattern = re.compile(rf"(?<![A-Za-zÀ-ÿ]){kw_regex}(?![A-Za-zÀ-ÿ])", re.IGNORECASE)
        all_locs: Set[str] = self._expand_saint_locations()
        glued_map: Dict[str, str] = dict(self.glued_to_alias)
        for loc in all_locs:
            compact_loc: str = self.normalize_ocr_key(loc)
            if len(compact_loc) >= 10 and compact_loc not in glued_map:
                glued_map[compact_loc] = "[LIEU_MASQUE]"
        self.glued_to_alias = glued_map
        if self.glued_to_alias:
            glued_alt: str = DocumentTextUtils.build_ocr_tolerant_trie_regex(list(self.glued_to_alias.keys()))
            self.combined_glued_pattern = re.compile(glued_alt, re.IGNORECASE)
        valid_locs: List[str] = [loc for loc in all_locs if len(loc) >= 3]
        if valid_locs:
            loc_regex: str = DocumentTextUtils.build_ocr_tolerant_trie_regex(valid_locs)
            self.combined_location_pattern = re.compile(rf"(?<![A-Za-zÀ-ÿ]){loc_regex}(?![A-Za-zÀ-ÿ])", re.IGNORECASE)

    def lookup_keyword_alias(self, matched_text: str) -> str:
        """Retourne l'alias correspondant à un mot-clé ou sa variante OCR."""
        low: str = matched_text.lower()
        if low in self.keyword_to_alias:
            return self.keyword_to_alias[low]
        norm_ocr: str = self.normalize_ocr_key(matched_text)
        for k, v in self.keyword_to_alias.items():
            if self.normalize_ocr_key(k) == norm_ocr:
                return v
        return matched_text

    def lookup_glued_alias(self, matched_text: str) -> str:
        """Retourne l'alias correspondant à un identifiant collé sans espaces."""
        compact: str = self.normalize_ocr_key(matched_text)
        if compact in self.glued_to_alias:
            return self.glued_to_alias[compact]
        for k, v in self.glued_to_alias.items():
            if self.normalize_ocr_key(k) == compact:
                return v
        return matched_text

    def load_all(self) -> bool:
        """Charge toutes les sources de règles et compile les expressions régulières."""
        if not self.manual_only and not os.path.exists(self.config.contacts_file):
            return False
        self._load_file_rules(self.config.contacts_file, is_manual=False)
        self._load_indexed_and_anonymized_pairs()
        self._load_file_rules(self.config.indexed_contacts_file, is_manual=False)
        self._load_file_rules(self.config.anonymized_contacts_file, is_manual=False)
        self._load_file_rules(self.config.discovered_contacts_file, is_manual=False)
        self._load_file_rules(self.config.manual_contacts_file, is_manual=True)
        self._register_protected_surnames()
        self._compile_patterns()
        return bool(self.contacts_id or self.keyword_to_alias or self.glued_to_alias or self.combined_location_pattern)

================================================================================
FICHIER : doc_anonymizer/discovered_contacts_builder.py
================================================================================
# Commandes pip à exécuter : Aucune dépendance externe requise

import os
import re
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Set, Tuple
from doc_anonymizer.config_manager import PipelineConfig
from doc_anonymizer.text_utils import DocumentTextUtils


class DiscoveredEmailContactsBuilder:
    """
    Génère et enrichit le fichier contacts_emails_decouverts.txt à partir
    de tous_emails_trouves.txt (adresses et noms d'affichage MIME) pour compléter
    contacts.txt et contacts_manuels.txt sans doublon.
    """

    def __init__(self, config: PipelineConfig) -> None:
        """
        Initialise le générateur de contacts découverts.

        :param config: Instance de PipelineConfig.
        """
        self.config: PipelineConfig = config

    def _collect_existing_targets(self) -> Set[str]:
        """
        Lit contacts.txt et contacts_manuels.txt afin de recenser toutes
        les cibles (e-mails ou noms) déjà couvertes.

        :return: Ensemble des cibles normalisées déjà définies.
        """
        covered: Set[str] = set()
        for path in (
            self.config.contacts_file,
            self.config.manual_contacts_file,
        ):
            if not os.path.exists(path):
                continue
            with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                for raw_line in f:
                    line: str = raw_line.strip()
                    if not line or line.startswith("#"):
                        continue
                    for sep in ("=", ";", ","):
                        if sep in line:
                            left_p, right_p = line.split(sep, 1)
                            target, alias, _ = (
                                DocumentTextUtils.resolve_contact_pair(
                                    left_p, right_p
                                )
                            )
                            if target and alias:
                                covered.add(
                                    DocumentTextUtils.normalize_identifier(
                                        target
                                    )
                                )
                                covered.add(
                                    DocumentTextUtils.normalize_word(target)
                                )
                            break
        return covered

    def _load_existing_discovered(self) -> Dict[str, str]:
        """
        Charge les règles déjà générées dans contacts_emails_decouverts.txt
        afin de préserver les alias existants entre deux exécutions.

        :return: Dictionnaire cible -> alias existant.
        """
        existing: Dict[str, str] = {}
        disc_path: str = self.config.discovered_contacts_file
        if not os.path.exists(disc_path):
            return existing
        with open(disc_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            for raw_line in f:
                line: str = raw_line.strip()
                if not line or line.startswith("#") or "=" not in line:
                    continue
                left_p, right_p = line.split("=", 1)
                target, alias, _ = DocumentTextUtils.resolve_contact_pair(
                    left_p, right_p
                )
                if target and alias:
                    existing[target] = alias
        return existing
    def _validate_display_name(self, raw_disp: str) -> Optional[str]:
        """
        Vérifie si un nom d'affichage MIME contient un prénom connu et un nom valide.

        :param raw_disp: Libellé brut issu d'un en-tête From/To.
        :return: 'Prénom Nom' normalisé ou None.
        """
        tokens: List[str] = [t for t in raw_disp.strip().split() if t]
        if len(tokens) != 2:
            return None
        t1, t2 = tokens
        if any(ch.isdigit() for ch in t1 + t2):
            return None
        n1: str = DocumentTextUtils.normalize_word(t1)
        n2: str = DocumentTextUtils.normalize_word(t2)
        if n1 in self.config.masked_locations or n2 in self.config.masked_locations:
            return None
        is_fn1: bool = n1 in self.config.known_first_names
        is_fn2: bool = n2 in self.config.known_first_names
        if is_fn1 and not is_fn2 and len(n2) >= 3:
            return f"{t1.capitalize()} {t2.capitalize()}"
        if is_fn2 and not is_fn1 and len(n1) >= 3:
            return f"{t2.capitalize()} {t1.capitalize()}"
        return None

    def _infer_full_name_from_email(self, email: str) -> Optional[str]:
        """
        Déduit 'Prénom Nom' si l'adresse e-mail est au format
        'prenom.nom@domaine' ou 'nom.prenom@domaine' avec un prénom connu.

        :param email: Adresse e-mail normalisée.
        :return: Chaîne 'Prénom Nom' capitalisée ou None.
        """
        if "@" not in email:
            return None
        local_part, _ = email.split("@", 1)
        tokens: List[str] = [
            t for t in re.split(r"[\.\-_]+", local_part.strip()) if t
        ]
        if len(tokens) != 2:
            return None
        first_tok, second_tok = tokens
        if any(ch.isdigit() for ch in first_tok + second_tok):
            return None
        if len(first_tok) < 2 or len(second_tok) < 2:
            return None

        norm_1: str = DocumentTextUtils.normalize_word(first_tok)
        norm_2: str = DocumentTextUtils.normalize_word(second_tok)
        is_fn_1: bool = norm_1 in self.config.known_first_names
        is_fn_2: bool = norm_2 in self.config.known_first_names

        if is_fn_1 and not is_fn_2:
            return f"{first_tok.capitalize()} {second_tok.capitalize()}"
        if is_fn_2 and not is_fn_1:
            return f"{second_tok.capitalize()} {first_tok.capitalize()}"
        return None

    def _next_alias_index(self, existing_rules: Dict[str, str]) -> int:
        """
        Détermine le prochain numéro disponible pour les alias Contact_Mail_XXX.

        :param existing_rules: Dictionnaire des règles déjà présentes.
        :return: Prochain index entier >= 1.
        """
        max_idx: int = 0
        for alias in existing_rules.values():
            match = re.match(r"^Contact_Mail_(\d+)$", alias.strip())
            if match:
                max_idx = max(max_idx, int(match.group(1)))
        return max_idx + 1

    @staticmethod
    def _sort_discovered_item(item: Tuple[str, str]) -> Tuple[str, str]:
        """
        Calcule la clé de tri alphabétique pour une entrée découverte.

        :param item: Tuple (cible, alias).
        :return: Tuple normalisé pour le tri.
        """
        target_key, _ = item
        return (
            DocumentTextUtils.normalize_word(target_key),
            target_key.lower(),
        )
    def build_or_update(self) -> Tuple[int, int]:
        """
        Lit tous_emails_trouves.txt, complète contacts_emails_decouverts.txt
        avec les adresses et noms déduits (via e-mail ou en-tête MIME),
        puis purge tous_emails_trouves.txt de façon atomique.

        :return: Tuple (nouvelles_regles_ajoutees, total_regles_fichier).
        """
        emails_path: str = self.config.all_emails_file
        if not os.path.exists(emails_path):
            return 0, 0

        covered_targets: Set[str] = self._collect_existing_targets()
        discovered_rules: Dict[str, str] = self._load_existing_discovered()
        existing_norm_keys: Set[str] = {
            DocumentTextUtils.normalize_identifier(k)
            for k in discovered_rules.keys()
        } | {
            DocumentTextUtils.normalize_word(k)
            for k in discovered_rules.keys()
        }

        email_to_display: Dict[str, str] = {}
        with open(emails_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f_mail:
            for raw_ln in f_mail:
                ln: str = raw_ln.strip()
                if "@" not in ln:
                    continue
                if "|" in ln:
                    raw_em, raw_disp = ln.split("|", 1)
                    norm_em: str = DocumentTextUtils.normalize_identifier(raw_em)
                    valid_disp: Optional[str] = self._validate_display_name(raw_disp)
                    if norm_em:
                        email_to_display.setdefault(norm_em, valid_disp or "")
                        if valid_disp and not email_to_display[norm_em]:
                            email_to_display[norm_em] = valid_disp
                else:
                    norm_em = DocumentTextUtils.normalize_identifier(ln)
                    if norm_em:
                        email_to_display.setdefault(norm_em, "")

        next_idx: int = self._next_alias_index(discovered_rules)
        added_count: int = 0

        for email, header_name in sorted(email_to_display.items()):
            alias: str = ""
            if email not in covered_targets and email not in existing_norm_keys:
                alias = f"Contact_Mail_{next_idx:03d}"
                next_idx += 1
                discovered_rules[email] = alias
                existing_norm_keys.add(email)
                added_count += 1
            else:
                alias = discovered_rules.get(email, "")

            inferred_name: Optional[str] = header_name or self._infer_full_name_from_email(email)
            if inferred_name and alias:
                norm_name: str = DocumentTextUtils.normalize_word(inferred_name)
                if norm_name not in covered_targets and norm_name not in existing_norm_keys:
                    discovered_rules[inferred_name] = alias
                    existing_norm_keys.add(norm_name)
                    added_count += 1

        if added_count > 0 or not os.path.exists(
            self.config.discovered_contacts_file
        ):
            out_path: str = self.config.discovered_contacts_file
            sorted_items: List[Tuple[str, str]] = sorted(
                discovered_rules.items(),
                key=self._sort_discovered_item,
            )
            out_lines: List[str] = [
                "# CONTACTS DÉCOUVERTS AUTOMATIQUEMENT DEPUIS "
                "tous_emails_trouves.txt\n",
                "# Complète contacts.txt et contacts_manuels.txt.\n\n",
            ]
            for target_k, alias_v in sorted_items:
                out_lines.append(f"{target_k} = {alias_v}\n")
            DocumentTextUtils.atomic_write_text(out_path, "".join(out_lines))

        DocumentTextUtils.atomic_write_text(emails_path, "")

        return added_count, len(discovered_rules)

================================================================================
FICHIER : doc_anonymizer/extraction_runner.py
================================================================================
# Commandes pip à exécuter : Aucune dépendance externe requise

from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor, as_completed
import os
import re
import threading
import time
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Set, Tuple
from doc_anonymizer.comm_extractors import CommunicationExtractor
from doc_anonymizer.config_manager import PipelineConfig
from doc_anonymizer.office_extractors import OfficeAndDocumentExtractor
from doc_anonymizer.text_utils import DocumentTextUtils


class DocumentExtractionRunner:
    """
    Orchestre l'étape 1 : inventaire des fichiers sources, extraction
    multi-formats parallèle et sauvegarde dans transcriptions/.
    """

    AUDIO_EXTS: Tuple[str, ...] = (
        ".wav", ".mp3", ".m4a", ".ogg", ".flac", ".aac", ".amr",
    )
    IMAGE_EXTS: Tuple[str, ...] = (
        ".jpg", ".jpeg", ".png", ".bmp", ".webp", ".tif", ".tiff",
    )
    HTML_EXTS: Tuple[str, ...] = (".html", ".htm")

    def __init__(
        self,
        config: PipelineConfig,
        stop_event: Optional[threading.Event] = None,
    ) -> None:
        """
        Initialise l'orchestrateur d'extraction et les verrous de concurrence.

        :param config: Instance de PipelineConfig.
        :param stop_event: Événement optionnel signalant un arrêt Ctrl+C.
        """
        self.config: PipelineConfig = config
        self.stop_event: threading.Event = stop_event or threading.Event()
        self.doc_extractor: OfficeAndDocumentExtractor = (
            OfficeAndDocumentExtractor(enable_ocr=config.enable_ocr)
        )
        self.comm_extractor: CommunicationExtractor = CommunicationExtractor(
            config=config, doc_extractor=self.doc_extractor
        )
        self.extraction_errors: List[str] = []
        self._io_lock: threading.Lock = threading.Lock()

    def get_worker_count(self) -> int:
        """
        Détermine le nombre optimal de threads pour l'extraction parallèle.

        :return: Nombre de workers (>= 1).
        """
        configured: int = getattr(self.config, "extraction_workers", 0)
        if configured >= 1:
            return configured
        cpu_total: int = os.cpu_count() or 2
        return max(1, min(4, cpu_total))

    @staticmethod
    def load_tracking_set(tracking_path: str) -> Set[str]:
        """
        Charge les identifiants déjà extraits depuis un fichier de suivi.

        :param tracking_path: Chemin vers le fichier de suivi.
        :return: Ensemble des clés enregistrées.
        """
        if not os.path.exists(tracking_path):
            return set()
        with open(tracking_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            return {line.strip() for line in f if line.strip()}

    @staticmethod
    def append_tracking_entry(tracking_path: str, entry: str) -> None:
        """
        Ajoute une entrée dans le fichier de suivi.

        :param tracking_path: Chemin vers le fichier de suivi.
        :param entry: Identifiant unique traité.
        """
        parent_dir: str = os.path.dirname(tracking_path)
        if parent_dir:
            os.makedirs(parent_dir, exist_ok=True)
        with open(tracking_path, "a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            f.write(f"{entry}\n")
    @classmethod
    def categorize_extension(cls, ext: str) -> str:
        """
        Détermine le sous-dossier thématique associé à l'extension.

        :param ext: Extension en minuscules avec point initial.
        :return: Nom de la catégorie.
        """
        if ext == ".pdf":
            return "PDF"
        if ext in cls.AUDIO_EXTS or ext == ".aup3":
            return "Audio"
        if ext in cls.IMAGE_EXTS:
            return "Images"
        if ext in cls.HTML_EXTS:
            return "Web"
        if ext in {".mbox", ".eml"}:
            return "Emails"
        if ext == ".xml":
            return "SMS"
        if ext == ".ics":
            return "Agendas"
        return "Documents"

    @staticmethod
    def _sanitize_filesystem_stem(stem: str) -> str:
        """
        Nettoie uniquement les caractères interdits par le système de fichiers.

        :param stem: Nom de fichier sans extension.
        :return: Nom de fichier valide sur disque.
        """
        cleaned: str = re.sub(r'[<>:"/\\|?*]', "_", stem).strip()
        return cleaned or "document_sans_nom"

    def _resolve_input_dir(self) -> str:
        """
        Résout le chemin complet du répertoire contenant les fichiers sources.

        :return: Chemin normalisé du répertoire d'entrée.
        """
        if os.path.isabs(self.config.sources_dir):
            return os.path.normpath(self.config.sources_dir)
        return os.path.normpath(os.path.join(self.config.root_dir, self.config.sources_dir))

    def _file_priority_key(self, file_path: str) -> Tuple[int, int, str]:
        """
        Calcule une clé de priorité pour reporter les fichiers lourds à la fin.

        :param file_path: Chemin complet du fichier candidat.
        :return: Tuple (groupe_priorite, taille_octets, chemin_normalise).
        """
        if not self.config.defer_heavy_files:
            return (0, 0, file_path)
        _, ext_raw = os.path.splitext(file_path)
        ext: str = ext_raw.lower()
        try:
            size_bytes: int = os.path.getsize(file_path)
        except OSError:
            size_bytes = 0
        threshold_bytes: int = int(self.config.heavy_file_threshold_mb * 1024 * 1024)
        is_heavy_type: bool = ext in {".mbox", ".aup3"} or ext in self.AUDIO_EXTS
        return (1, size_bytes, file_path) if (is_heavy_type or size_bytes >= threshold_bytes) else (0, 0, file_path)

    def collect_source_files(self) -> List[str]:
        """
        Parcourt le répertoire d'entrée configuré et ordonne les fichiers.

        :return: Liste ordonnée des chemins de fichiers à analyser.
        """
        input_dir: str = self._resolve_input_dir()
        if not os.path.isdir(input_dir):
            os.makedirs(input_dir, exist_ok=True)
            print(f"[AVERTISSEMENT] Dossier '{input_dir}' créé.", flush=True)
            return []

        collected: List[str] = []
        excluded_files: Set[str] = {
            os.path.basename(self.config.first_names_file),
            os.path.basename(self.config.locations_file),
            os.path.basename(self.config.excluded_words_file),
            os.path.basename(self.config.contacts_file),
            os.path.basename(self.config.manual_contacts_file),
            os.path.basename(self.config.discovered_contacts_file),
            os.path.basename(self.config.all_sms_numbers_file),
            os.path.basename(self.config.all_emails_file),
            os.path.basename(self.config.audit_report_file),
            os.path.basename(self.config.audit_copy_file),
        }
        for root_path, dirs, files in os.walk(input_dir):
            dirs[:] = sorted(d for d in dirs if d not in self.config.ignored_dirs and not d.startswith("."))
            for file_name in sorted(files):
                if file_name.startswith(".") or file_name in excluded_files or file_name.endswith((".py", ".ini")):
                    continue
                collected.append(os.path.join(root_path, file_name))
        collected.sort(key=self._file_priority_key)
        return collected
    def _write_transcription_block(self, period: str, category: str, raw_base_name: str, content: str) -> str:
        """
        Écrit de façon thread-safe le contenu extrait dans transcriptions/AAAA-MM/Catégorie/.

        :param period: Période AAAA-MM.
        :param category: Catégorie thématique du document.
        :param raw_base_name: Nom de fichier d'origine.
        :param content: Texte extrait prêt à enregistrer.
        :return: Chemin du fichier transcription généré.
        """
        target_dir: str = os.path.join(self.config.transcriptions_dir, period, category)
        out_path: str = os.path.join(target_dir, f"{raw_base_name}.txt")
        with self._io_lock:
            os.makedirs(target_dir, exist_ok=True)
            with open(out_path, "a", encoding="utf-8") as f_out:
                f_out.write(content)
        return out_path

    def process_single_file(
        self, file_path: str, target_phones: Dict[str, str], allowed_emails: Set[str],
        already_extracted: Set[str], sms_processed: Set[str],
        discovered_phones: Set[str], discovered_emails: Set[str],
    ) -> List[str]:
        """
        Traite un fichier source (compatible exécution parallèle) et retourne les transcriptions.

        :param file_path: Chemin vers le fichier candidat.
        :param target_phones: Téléphones issus des fichiers de contacts.
        :param allowed_emails: E-mails issus des fichiers de contacts.
        :param already_extracted: Fichiers déjà traités.
        :param sms_processed: Clés SMS déjà extraites.
        :param discovered_phones: Collecteur des numéros rencontrés.
        :param discovered_emails: Collecteur des e-mails rencontrés.
        :return: Liste des chemins de transcriptions générées.
        """
        if self.stop_event.is_set():
            return []

        input_dir: str = self._resolve_input_dir()
        norm_rel: str = os.path.normpath(os.path.relpath(file_path, input_dir))
        file_name: str = os.path.basename(file_path)
        stem, ext_raw = os.path.splitext(file_name)
        ext: str = ext_raw.lower()
        raw_stem: str = self._sanitize_filesystem_stem(stem)
        category: str = self.categorize_extension(ext)
        generated_paths: List[str] = []

        if ext == ".xml":
            local_phones: Set[str] = set()
            local_emails: Set[str] = set()
            with self._io_lock:
                sms_snapshot: Set[str] = set(sms_processed)
            sms_by_month, xml_errors = self.comm_extractor.parse_sms_xml(
                file_path, target_phones, sms_snapshot, local_phones, local_emails
            )
            if self.stop_event.is_set():
                return []
            with self._io_lock:
                discovered_phones.update(local_phones)
                discovered_emails.update(local_emails)
                self.extraction_errors.extend(xml_errors)
            if sms_by_month:
                for period, entries in sorted(sms_by_month.items()):
                    entries.sort(key=lambda item: item)
                    merged_sms: str = "".join(block for _, block in entries)
                    generated_paths.append(self._write_transcription_block(period, category, raw_stem, merged_sms))
                with self._io_lock:
                    for num in local_phones:
                        if num in target_phones:
                            key: str = f"{file_name}|{num}"
                            if key not in sms_processed:
                                self.append_tracking_entry(self.config.sms_tracking_file, key)
                                sms_processed.add(key)
            return generated_paths

        with self._io_lock:
            if norm_rel in already_extracted:
                return []

        if ext in {".mbox", ".eml"}:
            local_mail_disc: Set[str] = set()
            emails_by_month, err = self.comm_extractor.extract_emails(file_path, allowed_emails, local_mail_disc)
            if self.stop_event.is_set():
                return []
            with self._io_lock:
                discovered_emails.update(local_mail_disc)
            if err:
                with self._io_lock:
                    self.extraction_errors.append(f"{norm_rel} -> {err}")
                return []
            if emails_by_month:
                for period, blocks in sorted(emails_by_month.items()):
                    generated_paths.append(self._write_transcription_block(period, category, raw_stem, "".join(blocks)))
                with self._io_lock:
                    self.append_tracking_entry(self.config.extraction_tracking_file, norm_rel)
                    already_extracted.add(norm_rel)
            return generated_paths
        extractors_map = {
            ".pdf": lambda p: self.doc_extractor.extract_pdf(p, file_name),
            ".txt": self.doc_extractor.extract_text_or_markdown,
            ".md": self.doc_extractor.extract_text_or_markdown,
            ".rtf": self.doc_extractor.extract_rtf,
            ".docx": self.doc_extractor.extract_docx,
            ".odt": self.doc_extractor.extract_odt,
            ".xlsx": self.doc_extractor.extract_xlsx,
            ".pptx": self.doc_extractor.extract_pptx,
            ".ics": self.doc_extractor.extract_ics,
            ".aup3": self.comm_extractor.extract_audacity_aup3,
        }
        if ext in self.HTML_EXTS:
            extracted_text, err_msg = self.doc_extractor.extract_html(file_path)
        elif ext in self.IMAGE_EXTS:
            extracted_text, err_msg = self.doc_extractor.extract_image(file_path)
        elif ext in self.AUDIO_EXTS:
            extracted_text, err_msg = self.comm_extractor.extract_audio(file_path)
        elif ext in extractors_map:
            extracted_text, err_msg = extractors_map[ext](file_path)
        else:
            return []

        if self.stop_event.is_set():
            return []

        if err_msg or not extracted_text:
            if err_msg:
                with self._io_lock:
                    self.extraction_errors.append(f"{norm_rel} -> {err_msg}")
            return []

        period = DocumentTextUtils.resolve_file_period(extracted_text, file_name, file_path)
        out_p: str = self._write_transcription_block(period, category, raw_stem, extracted_text)
        with self._io_lock:
            self.append_tracking_entry(self.config.extraction_tracking_file, norm_rel)
            already_extracted.add(norm_rel)
        return [out_p]

    def load_all_filter_contacts(self) -> Tuple[Optional[Dict[str, str]], Optional[Set[str]]]:
        """
        Fusionne les téléphones et e-mails des trois fichiers de contacts.

        :return: Tuple (dictionnaire_telephones, ensemble_emails) ou (None, None).
        """
        target_phones, allowed_emails = DocumentTextUtils.load_filter_contacts(self.config.contacts_file)
        if target_phones is None or allowed_emails is None:
            return None, None
        for extra_path in (self.config.discovered_contacts_file, self.config.manual_contacts_file):
            if os.path.exists(extra_path):
                ex_phones, ex_emails = DocumentTextUtils.load_filter_contacts(extra_path)
                if ex_phones:
                    target_phones.update(ex_phones)
                if ex_emails:
                    allowed_emails.update(ex_emails)
        return target_phones, allowed_emails

    def finalize_reports(self, discovered_phones: Set[str], discovered_emails: Set[str]) -> None:
        """
        Écrit les rapports d'identifiants rencontrés et d'erreurs d'extraction.

        :param discovered_phones: Numéros rencontrés pendant l'extraction.
        :param discovered_emails: E-mails rencontrés pendant l'extraction.
        """
        existing_sms: Set[str] = self.load_tracking_set(self.config.all_sms_numbers_file)
        expanded: Set[str] = set()
        for raw_entry in existing_sms | discovered_phones:
            expanded.update(DocumentTextUtils.split_sms_addresses(raw_entry))

        clean_phones: Set[str] = {item for item in expanded if "@" not in item}
        migrated_emails: Set[str] = {
            item for item in expanded if "@" in item and not item.lower().endswith("://google.com")
        }
        if clean_phones or os.path.exists(self.config.all_sms_numbers_file):
            with open(self.config.all_sms_numbers_file, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f_num:
                f_num.writelines(f"{num}\n" for num in sorted(clean_phones))

        merged_emails: Set[str] = self.load_tracking_set(self.config.all_emails_file) | discovered_emails | migrated_emails
        if merged_emails:
            with open(self.config.all_emails_file, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f_mail:
                f_mail.writelines(f"{mail}\n" for mail in sorted(merged_emails))

        if self.extraction_errors:
            report_path: str = os.path.join(self.config.transcriptions_dir, "rapport_extraction.txt")
            with open(report_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f_err:
                f_err.writelines(f"{err}\n" for err in self.extraction_errors)

    def run(self) -> None:
        """
        Exécute la phase autonome d'extraction multi-formats en parallèle.
        """
        start_time: float = time.time()
        workers: int = self.get_worker_count()
        print(f"=== ÉTAPE 1 : EXTRACTION MULTI-FORMATS ({workers} worker(s)) ===", flush=True)
        target_phones, allowed_emails = self.load_all_filter_contacts()
        if target_phones is None or allowed_emails is None:
            return

        os.makedirs(self.config.transcriptions_dir, exist_ok=True)
        already_extracted: Set[str] = self.load_tracking_set(self.config.extraction_tracking_file)
        sms_processed: Set[str] = self.load_tracking_set(self.config.sms_tracking_file)
        discovered_phones: Set[str] = set()
        discovered_emails: Set[str] = set()

        files: List[str] = self.collect_source_files()
        total: int = len(files)
        print(f"Inventaire : {total} fichier(s) candidat(s).", flush=True)
        processed_count: int = 0
        executor: ThreadPoolExecutor = ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=workers)

        try:
            futures = {
                executor.submit(
                    self.process_single_file,
                    fp, target_phones, allowed_emails, already_extracted,
                    sms_processed, discovered_phones, discovered_emails
                ): fp
                for fp in files
            }
            for idx, future in enumerate(as_completed(futures), start=1):
                fp: str = futures[future]
                print(f"[{idx}/{total}] Terminé : {os.path.basename(fp)}", flush=True)
                if future.result():
                    processed_count += 1
            executor.shutdown(wait=True)
        except KeyboardInterrupt:
            self.stop_event.set()
            executor.shutdown(wait=False, cancel_futures=True)
            raise

        self.finalize_reports(discovered_phones, discovered_emails)
        print(f"\nExtraction terminée : {processed_count} fichier(s) en {time.time() - start_time:.1f}s.", flush=True)

================================================================================
FICHIER : doc_anonymizer/manual_contacts_importer.py
================================================================================
# Commandes pip à exécuter : Aucune dépendance externe requise

import os
import re
from typing import Dict, List, Set, Tuple
from doc_anonymizer.config_manager import PipelineConfig
from doc_anonymizer.contact_rules_loader import ContactRulesLoader
from doc_anonymizer.text_utils import DocumentTextUtils


class ManualContactsAutoCompleter:
    """
    Analyse les détections haute confiance de l'audit des noms résiduels
    et les importe automatiquement dans contacts_manuels.txt sans doublon.
    """

    AUDIT_LINE_PATTERN = re.compile(
        r"^\s*([A-Za-zÉÈÊËÀÂÄÎÏÔÖÙÛÜÇéèêëàâäîïôöùûüç'\-\s]+?)\s*=\s*"
        r"([A-Za-zÉÈÊËÀÂÄÎÏÔÖÙÛÜÇéèêëàâäîïôöùûüç'\-\.\s_]+?)"
        r"(?:\s*#\s*\[\s*(\d+)\s*x\s*\].*|\s*#\s*(\d+)\s*occ\.)?\s*$"
    )

    def __init__(
        self,
        config: PipelineConfig,
        rules: ContactRulesLoader,
        min_occurrences: int = 3,
    ) -> None:
        """Initialise l'importateur automatique depuis le rapport d'audit."""
        self.config: PipelineConfig = config
        self.rules: ContactRulesLoader = rules
        self.min_occurrences: int = min_occurrences

    def _load_existing_manual_keys(self) -> Tuple[Set[str], List[str]]:
        """Charge les cibles déjà présentes dans contacts_manuels.txt."""
        existing_keys: Set[str] = set()
        lines: List[str] = []
        path: str = self.config.manual_contacts_file
        if not os.path.exists(path):
            return existing_keys, lines
        with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            for raw_line in f:
                lines.append(raw_line.rstrip("\r\n"))
                stripped: str = raw_line.strip()
                if not stripped or stripped.startswith("#"):
                    continue
                if "=" in stripped:
                    left, _ = stripped.split("=", 1)
                    existing_keys.add(DocumentTextUtils.normalize_word(left))
        return existing_keys, lines

    def _is_valid_import_candidate(self, name: str, alias: str) -> bool:
        """Vérifie qu'un nom issu de l'audit est éligible à l'import automatique."""
        if not name or not alias:
            return False
        words: List[str] = [w for w in name.split() if w]
        if len(words) < 2:
            return False
        for w in words:
            norm: str = DocumentTextUtils.normalize_word(w)
            if (
                norm in self.config.excluded_words
                or norm in self.config.masked_locations
                or norm in DocumentTextUtils.ORG_GENERIC_WORDS
            ):
                return False
        return True
    def _format_default_alias(self, name: str) -> str:
        """Génère un alias 'Prénom I.' par défaut si l'audit indique A_MASQUER."""
        tokens: List[str] = [t for t in name.split() if t]
        if len(tokens) >= 2:
            first_tok: str = tokens[0]
            return f"{first_tok.capitalize()} {tokens[-1][:1].upper()}."
        return f"{name[:1].upper()}."

    def import_high_confidence_from_audit(self) -> int:
        """Importe dans contacts_manuels.txt les noms dépassant min_occurrences."""
        source_path: str = (
            self.config.audit_report_file
            if os.path.exists(self.config.audit_report_file)
            else self.config.audit_copy_file
        )
        if not os.path.exists(source_path):
            return 0

        existing_keys, lines = self._load_existing_manual_keys()
        new_entries: Dict[str, str] = {}

        with open(source_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            for raw_line in f:
                stripped: str = raw_line.strip()
                if not stripped or stripped.startswith("#"):
                    continue
                m = self.AUDIT_LINE_PATTERN.match(stripped)
                if not m:
                    continue
                name: str = m.group(1).strip()
                raw_alias: str = m.group(2).strip()
                occ_str: str = m.group(3) or m.group(4) or "0"
                occ: int = int(occ_str)
                if occ < self.min_occurrences:
                    continue
                norm_name: str = DocumentTextUtils.normalize_word(name)
                if norm_name in existing_keys or norm_name in new_entries:
                    continue
                alias: str = (
                    self._format_default_alias(name)
                    if raw_alias == "A_MASQUER"
                    else raw_alias
                )
                if self._is_valid_import_candidate(name, alias):
                    new_entries[norm_name] = f"{name} = {alias}"

        if not new_entries:
            return 0

        if lines and lines[-1].strip():
            lines.append("")
        lines.extend(sorted(new_entries.values(), key=str.lower))
        DocumentTextUtils.atomic_write_text(
            self.config.manual_contacts_file,
            "\n".join(lines) + "\n",
        )
        return len(new_entries)

================================================================================
FICHIER : doc_anonymizer/office_extractors.py
================================================================================
# Commandes pip à exécuter :
# pip install pymupdf rapidocr-onnxruntime python-docx odfpy striprtf openpyxl python-pptx

import os
import re
from typing import Any, List, Optional, Tuple
from docx import Document as DocxDocument
from odf import teletype
from odf.opendocument import load as load_odt
from odf.text import P as OdfP
import openpyxl
from pptx import Presentation
import pymupdf
from rapidocr_onnxruntime import RapidOCR
from striprtf.striprtf import rtf_to_text
from doc_anonymizer.config_manager import PipelineConfig
from doc_anonymizer.text_utils import DocumentTextUtils


class OfficeAndDocumentExtractor:
    """
    Gère l'extraction de texte pour les formats PDF (natif + OCR paresseux),
    bureautiques (.docx, .odt, .rtf, .xlsx, .pptx), web (.html), texte brut,
    images et calendriers (.ics).
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        config: Optional[PipelineConfig] = None,
        enable_ocr: bool = True,
    ) -> None:
        """
        Initialise l'extracteur sans charger immédiatement RapidOCR en RAM.

        :param config: Instance optionnelle de PipelineConfig.
        :param enable_ocr: Active ou désactive l'OCR si config n'est pas fourni.
        """
        self.config: Optional[PipelineConfig] = config
        self.enable_ocr: bool = (
            config.enable_ocr if config is not None else enable_ocr
        )
        self._ocr_engine: Optional[RapidOCR] = None

    @property
    def ocr_engine(self) -> Optional[RapidOCR]:
        """
        Charge le moteur RapidOCR uniquement à la première image ou page PDF
        scannée rencontrée.

        :return: Instance de RapidOCR ou None si l'OCR est désactivé.
        """
        if not self.enable_ocr:
            return None
        if self._ocr_engine is None:
            print("Chargement du moteur OCR (RapidOCR)...", flush=True)
            self._ocr_engine = RapidOCR()
            print("[OK] Moteur OCR chargé.", flush=True)
        return self._ocr_engine

    def run_ocr_on_bytes(self, image_bytes: bytes) -> str:
        """
        Exécute la reconnaissance optique de caractères sur un tampon binaire.

        :param image_bytes: Données binaires de l'image (PNG/JPEG).
        :return: Texte reconnu ligne par ligne.
        """
        engine: Optional[RapidOCR] = self.ocr_engine
        if not engine:
            return ""
        result, _ = engine(image_bytes)
        if not result:
            return ""
        return "\n".join(item for item in result if len(item) > 1)
    def extract_pdf(
        self, pdf_source: Any, display_name: str, is_stream: bool = False
    ) -> Tuple[Optional[str], Optional[str]]:
        """
        Extrait le texte d'un document PDF page par page avec bascule RapidOCR.

        :param pdf_source: Chemin fichier (str) ou flux binaire (bytes).
        :param display_name: Nom affiché dans l'en-tête.
        :param is_stream: Indique si pdf_source est un flux bytes.
        :return: Tuple (texte_formate, message_erreur).
        """
        doc: Optional[Any] = None
        try:
            doc = (
                pymupdf.open(stream=pdf_source, filetype="pdf")
                if is_stream
                else pymupdf.open(pdf_source)
            )
            total_pages: int = len(doc)
            pages_text: List[str] = []
            for page_idx in range(total_pages):
                page = doc[page_idx]
                native_txt: str = page.get_text("text").strip()
                if len(native_txt) >= 30:
                    pages_text.append(f"--- Page {page_idx + 1} ---\n{native_txt}")
                elif self.enable_ocr:
                    print(f"    [OCR] {display_name} : page {page_idx + 1}/{total_pages}...", flush=True)
                    pix = page.get_pixmap(dpi=150)
                    ocr_txt: str = self.run_ocr_on_bytes(pix.tobytes("png"))
                    if ocr_txt.strip():
                        pages_text.append(f"--- Page {page_idx + 1} (OCR) ---\n{ocr_txt.strip()}")
            if not pages_text:
                return None, "PDF vide ou illisible"
            header: str = f"========================================\nDOCUMENT PDF : {display_name}\n----------------------------------------\n"
            return header + "\n\n".join(pages_text) + "\n\n", None
        except Exception as exc:
            return None, f"Erreur extraction PDF ({exc})"
        finally:
            if doc is not None:
                try:
                    doc.close()
                except Exception:
                    pass

    def extract_image(self, file_path: str) -> Tuple[Optional[str], Optional[str]]:
        """
        Extrait le texte d'une image via RapidOCR.

        :param file_path: Chemin vers le fichier image.
        :return: Tuple (texte_formate, message_erreur).
        """
        if not self.enable_ocr:
            return None, "OCR désactivé dans config.ini"
        file_name: str = os.path.basename(file_path)
        try:
            print(f"    [OCR] Image : {file_name}...", flush=True)
            with open(file_path, "rb") as f_img:
                ocr_txt: str = self.run_ocr_on_bytes(f_img.read()).strip()
            if not ocr_txt:
                return None, "Aucun texte détecté dans l'image"
            header: str = f"========================================\nIMAGE (OCR) : {file_name}\n----------------------------------------\n"
            return header + ocr_txt + "\n\n", None
        except Exception as exc:
            return None, f"Erreur OCR image ({exc})"
    def extract_docx(self, file_path: str) -> Tuple[Optional[str], Optional[str]]:
        """
        Extrait les paragraphes et tableaux d'un document Word (.docx).

        :param file_path: Chemin vers le fichier .docx.
        :return: Tuple (texte_formate, message_erreur).
        """
        file_name: str = os.path.basename(file_path)
        try:
            doc = DocxDocument(file_path)
            lines: List[str] = [p.text.strip() for p in doc.paragraphs if p.text.strip()]
            for table in doc.tables:
                for row in table.rows:
                    cells: List[str] = [c.text.strip().replace("\n", " ") for c in row.cells]
                    if any(cells):
                        lines.append(" | ".join(cells))
            if not lines:
                return None, "Document Word vide"
            header: str = f"========================================\nDOCUMENT WORD : {file_name}\n----------------------------------------\n"
            return header + "\n".join(lines) + "\n\n", None
        except Exception as exc:
            return None, f"Erreur extraction DOCX ({exc})"

    def extract_odt(self, file_path: str) -> Tuple[Optional[str], Optional[str]]:
        """
        Extrait le texte d'un document OpenDocument (.odt).

        :param file_path: Chemin vers le fichier .odt.
        :return: Tuple (texte_formate, message_erreur).
        """
        file_name: str = os.path.basename(file_path)
        try:
            doc = load_odt(file_path)
            lines: List[str] = [
                teletype.extractText(p).strip()
                for p in doc.getElementsByType(OdfP)
                if teletype.extractText(p).strip()
            ]
            if not lines:
                return None, "Document ODT vide"
            header: str = f"========================================\nDOCUMENT ODT : {file_name}\n----------------------------------------\n"
            return header + "\n".join(lines) + "\n\n", None
        except Exception as exc:
            return None, f"Erreur extraction ODT ({exc})"

    def extract_rtf(self, file_path: str) -> Tuple[Optional[str], Optional[str]]:
        """
        Extrait le texte d'un document Rich Text Format (.rtf).

        :param file_path: Chemin vers le fichier .rtf.
        :return: Tuple (texte_formate, message_erreur).
        """
        file_name: str = os.path.basename(file_path)
        try:
            with open(file_path, "r", encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore") as f:
                plain: str = rtf_to_text(f.read()).strip()
            if not plain:
                return None, "Document RTF vide"
            header: str = f"========================================\nDOCUMENT RTF : {file_name}\n----------------------------------------\n"
            return header + plain + "\n\n", None
        except Exception as exc:
            return None, f"Erreur extraction RTF ({exc})"
    @staticmethod
    def _format_excel_cell(val: Any) -> str:
        """
        Convertit proprement une cellule Excel en supprimant le suffixe '.0' des entiers.

        :param val: Valeur brute de la cellule.
        :return: Chaîne formatée.
        """
        if val is None:
            return ""
        if isinstance(val, float) and val.is_integer():
            return str(int(val))
        return str(val).strip()

    def extract_xlsx(self, file_path: str) -> Tuple[Optional[str], Optional[str]]:
        """
        Extrait le contenu des feuilles d'un classeur Excel (.xlsx).

        :param file_path: Chemin vers le fichier .xlsx.
        :return: Tuple (texte_formate, message_erreur).
        """
        file_name: str = os.path.basename(file_path)
        try:
            wb = openpyxl.load_workbook(file_path, data_only=True)
            sheets_out: List[str] = []
            for sheet_name in wb.sheetnames:
                ws = wb[sheet_name]
                rows_txt: List[str] = []
                for row in ws.iter_rows(values_only=True):
                    vals: List[str] = [self._format_excel_cell(v) for v in row]
                    if any(vals):
                        rows_txt.append(" | ".join(vals))
                if rows_txt:
                    sheets_out.append(f"--- Feuille : {sheet_name} ---\n" + "\n".join(rows_txt))
            wb.close()
            if not sheets_out:
                return None, "Classeur Excel vide"
            header: str = f"========================================\nCLASSEUR EXCEL : {file_name}\n----------------------------------------\n"
            return header + "\n\n".join(sheets_out) + "\n\n", None
        except Exception as exc:
            return None, f"Erreur extraction XLSX ({exc})"

    def extract_pptx(self, file_path: str) -> Tuple[Optional[str], Optional[str]]:
        """
        Extrait le texte des diapositives d'une présentation PowerPoint (.pptx).

        :param file_path: Chemin vers le fichier .pptx.
        :return: Tuple (texte_formate, message_erreur).
        """
        file_name: str = os.path.basename(file_path)
        try:
            prs = Presentation(file_path)
            slides_out: List[str] = []
            for idx, slide in enumerate(prs.slides, start=1):
                texts: List[str] = [sh.text.strip() for sh in slide.shapes if hasattr(sh, "text") and sh.text.strip()]
                if texts:
                    slides_out.append(f"--- Diapositive {idx} ---\n" + "\n".join(texts))
            if not slides_out:
                return None, "Présentation PPTX vide"
            header: str = f"========================================\nPRÉSENTATION PPTX : {file_name}\n----------------------------------------\n"
            return header + "\n\n".join(slides_out) + "\n\n", None
        except Exception as exc:
            return None, f"Erreur extraction PPTX ({exc})"

    def extract_html(self, file_path: str) -> Tuple[Optional[str], Optional[str]]:
        """
        Extrait le texte lisible d'une page web locale (.html / .htm).

        :param file_path: Chemin vers le fichier HTML.
        :return: Tuple (texte_formate, message_erreur).
        """
        file_name: str = os.path.basename(file_path)
        try:
            with open(file_path, "r", encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore") as f:
                plain: str = DocumentTextUtils.html_to_plain_text(f.read()).strip()
            if not plain:
                return None, "Page HTML vide"
            header: str = f"========================================\nPAGE WEB : {file_name}\n----------------------------------------\n"
            return header + plain + "\n\n", None
        except Exception as exc:
            return None, f"Erreur extraction HTML ({exc})"

    def extract_text_or_markdown(self, file_path: str) -> Tuple[Optional[str], Optional[str]]:
        """
        Extrait le contenu d'un fichier texte (.txt, .md).

        :param file_path: Chemin vers le fichier texte.
        :return: Tuple (texte_formate, message_erreur).
        """
        file_name: str = os.path.basename(file_path)
        try:
            with open(file_path, "r", encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore") as f:
                content: str = f.read().strip()
            if not content:
                return None, "Fichier texte vide"
            header: str = f"========================================\nTEXTE : {file_name}\n----------------------------------------\n"
            return header + content + "\n\n", None
        except Exception as exc:
            return None, f"Erreur lecture texte ({exc})"

    def extract_ics(self, file_path: str) -> Tuple[Optional[str], Optional[str]]:
        """
        Extrait les événements d'un agenda iCalendar (.ics).

        :param file_path: Chemin vers le fichier .ics.
        :return: Tuple (texte_formate, message_erreur).
        """
        file_name: str = os.path.basename(file_path)
        try:
            with open(file_path, "r", encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore") as f:
                unfolded: str = re.sub(r"\r?\n[ \t]", "", f.read())
            events: List[str] = []
            current_ev: List[str] = []
            in_event: bool = False
            for line in unfolded.splitlines():
                if line.startswith("BEGIN:VEVENT"):
                    in_event = True
                    current_ev = []
                elif line.startswith("END:VEVENT"):
                    in_event = False
                    if current_ev:
                        events.append("\n".join(current_ev))
                elif in_event and any(line.startswith(k) for k in ("DTSTART", "DTEND", "SUMMARY", "DESCRIPTION", "LOCATION")):
                    current_ev.append(line)
            if not events:
                return None, "Aucun événement ICS trouvé"
            header: str = f"========================================\nAGENDA ICS : {file_name}\n----------------------------------------\n"
            return header + "\n---\n".join(events) + "\n\n", None
        except Exception as exc:
            return None, f"Erreur extraction ICS ({exc})"

================================================================================
FICHIER : doc_anonymizer/pipeline_runner.py
================================================================================
# Commandes pip à exécuter : Aucune dépendance externe requise

from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor, as_completed
import os
import shutil
import threading
import time
from typing import List, Set
from doc_anonymizer.anonymization_runner import DocumentAnonymizationRunner
from doc_anonymizer.config_manager import PipelineConfig
from doc_anonymizer.discovered_contacts_builder import (
    DiscoveredEmailContactsBuilder,
)
from doc_anonymizer.extraction_runner import DocumentExtractionRunner
from doc_anonymizer.power_manager import SystemSleepInhibitor


class DocAnonymizerPipelineRunner:
    """
    Ordonnanceur parallèle au fil de l'eau pour doc_anonymizer : extrait les
    documents sur plusieurs threads et anonymise chaque transcription dès qu'elle
    est prête sur le thread principal, avec arrêt immédiat sur Ctrl+C.
    """

    def __init__(
        self, config: PipelineConfig, prevent_sleep: bool = True
    ) -> None:
        """
        Initialise l'ordonnanceur avec la configuration et le maintien d'éveil.

        :param config: Instance de PipelineConfig.
        :param prevent_sleep: Empêche la mise en veille OS durant l'exécution.
        """
        self.config: PipelineConfig = config
        self.prevent_sleep: bool = prevent_sleep
        self.stop_event: threading.Event = threading.Event()

    def _anonymize_pending_transcriptions(
        self, anonymizer: DocumentAnonymizationRunner
    ) -> int:
        """
        Parcourt le dossier transcriptions et anonymise immédiatement tout
        fichier déjà extrait mais absent de l'état d'anonymisation.

        :param anonymizer: Instance de DocumentAnonymizationRunner initialisée.
        :return: Nombre de transcriptions en attente anonymisées.
        """
        trans_dir: str = self.config.transcriptions_dir
        if not os.path.isdir(trans_dir):
            return 0

        pending_files: List[str] = []
        for root_path, _, existing_files in os.walk(trans_dir):
            for file_name in sorted(existing_files):
                if (
                    file_name == "rapport_extraction.txt"
                    or file_name.startswith(".")
                    or not file_name.lower().endswith(".txt")
                ):
                    continue
                full_trans: str = os.path.join(root_path, file_name)
                norm_trans: str = os.path.normpath(full_trans)
                if norm_trans not in anonymizer.audit.already_processed:
                    pending_files.append(full_trans)

        if not pending_files:
            return 0

        print(
            f"Rattrapage : {len(pending_files)} transcription(s) "
            "en attente d'anonymisation...",
            flush=True,
        )
        processed_count: int = 0
        for idx, trans_path in enumerate(pending_files, start=1):
            if self.stop_event.is_set():
                break
            if idx == 1 or idx % 25 == 0 or idx == len(pending_files):
                print(
                    f"    [Anonymisation {idx}/{len(pending_files)}] "
                    f"{os.path.basename(trans_path)}",
                    flush=True,
                )
            if anonymizer.anonymize_single_file(trans_path):
                processed_count += 1

        return processed_count
    def _reset_results_for_second_pass(self) -> None:
        """
        Nettoie intégralement les anciens lots et l'état JSON avant de relancer
        une passe complète avec les règles fraîchement découvertes.
        """
        res_dir: str = self.config.results_dir
        if os.path.isdir(res_dir):
            for entry in os.listdir(res_dir):
                full_p: str = os.path.join(res_dir, entry)
                if os.path.isdir(full_p) and entry.startswith("lot_"):
                    shutil.rmtree(full_p, ignore_errors=True)
                elif os.path.isfile(full_p) and entry.endswith(".txt"):
                    try:
                        os.remove(full_p)
                    except OSError:
                        pass
        if os.path.exists(self.config.resume_state_file):
            os.remove(self.config.resume_state_file)

    def _run_second_pass_with_discovered_emails(self) -> bool:
        """
        Génère contacts_emails_decouverts.txt depuis tous_emails_trouves.txt
        et exécute une seconde passe d'anonymisation si des règles ont été ajoutées.

        :return: True si une seconde passe a été exécutée, False sinon.
        """
        builder: DiscoveredEmailContactsBuilder = (
            DiscoveredEmailContactsBuilder(self.config)
        )
        added_count, total_count = builder.build_or_update()
        if added_count <= 0:
            return False

        pass_start: float = time.time()
        print(
            f"\n=== SECONDE PASSE D'ANONYMISATION ===\n"
            f"[CONTACTS DÉCOUVERTS] {added_count} nouvelle(s) règle(s) "
            f"ajoutée(s) dans '{self.config.discovered_contacts_file}' "
            f"(total : {total_count}).\n"
            "Relance complète de l'anonymisation sur les transcriptions...",
            flush=True,
        )
        self._reset_results_for_second_pass()
        second_anonymizer: DocumentAnonymizationRunner = (
            DocumentAnonymizationRunner(config=self.config)
        )
        if not second_anonymizer.initialize():
            return False

        self._anonymize_pending_transcriptions(second_anonymizer)
        second_anonymizer.finalize(pass_start)
        return True
    def run_all(self) -> None:
        """
        Exécute le rattrapage des transcriptions, l'extraction parallèle et
        l'anonymisation au fil de l'eau, avec arrêt immédiat sans blocage sur Ctrl+C.
        """
        start_time: float = time.perf_counter()
        wall_start: float = time.time()
        self.stop_event.clear()

        with SystemSleepInhibitor(enabled=self.prevent_sleep):
            print("=== PIPELINE DOC_ANONYMIZER (PARALLÈLE AU FIL DE L'EAU) ===", flush=True)
            print(f"[CONFIG] Fichier INI       : {self.config.config_path}", flush=True)
            print(f"[CONFIG] Contacts auto     : {self.config.contacts_file}", flush=True)
            print(f"[CONFIG] Contacts découverts : {self.config.discovered_contacts_file}", flush=True)
            print(f"[CONFIG] Contacts manuels  : {self.config.manual_contacts_file}", flush=True)
            print(f"[CONFIG] Sources           : {self.config.sources_dir}", flush=True)
            print(f"[CONFIG] Transcriptions    : {self.config.transcriptions_dir}", flush=True)
            print(f"[CONFIG] Résultats         : {self.config.results_dir}", flush=True)

            extractor: DocumentExtractionRunner = DocumentExtractionRunner(
                config=self.config, stop_event=self.stop_event
            )
            anonymizer: DocumentAnonymizationRunner = DocumentAnonymizationRunner(config=self.config)
            if not anonymizer.initialize():
                print("[ERREUR] Échec de l'initialisation de l'anonymiseur. Arrêt.", flush=True)
                return

            target_phones, allowed_emails = extractor.load_all_filter_contacts()
            if target_phones is None or allowed_emails is None:
